Method: 采用替代范式数据，让模型生成替换预先存在的人类对话中一方的轮次，测量相邻轮次间上下文无关文法（CFG）规则的重用情况。实验涵盖16个开放权重的Llama和Gemma模型（1B-70B，预训练和指令微调），在1,901个匹配位置上进行评估。将模型输出与前一个人类轮次（实际素材）和采样的无关人类轮次（随机素材）的CFG规则重叠度进行比较。同时比较指令微调模型与预训练模型，以及模型输出与被替代的人类回复的重叠度。探索性分析了词汇和语义相似度。

Result: 所有模型与实际前言的CFG规则重叠度均高于随机前言，且低频规则的差异更大。每个指令微调模型与实际前言的重叠度均高于其替代的人类回复，且所有8对匹配架构在指令微调后实际前言重叠度均增加。但指令微调模型与无关前言重叠度更高，实际与随机差异更小，且在控制目标规则集大小后条件规则重用几率更低。探索性分析显示，所有模型在词汇和语义相似度上均高于匹配的人类回复；指令微调模型在语义相似度上均高于预训练模型（8对架构均如此），词汇相似度结果则较为混杂。

Conclusion: 大语言模型表现出类似人类的句法趋同行为，但指令微调虽然增强了与实际语境的句法对齐，也增加了与无关语境的表面相似度，降低了条件规则重用的特异性。模型在词汇和语义层面的趋同程度甚至超过人类，指令微调普遍增强语义趋同，但对词汇趋同的影响不一。

Abstract: Syntactic convergence (the tendency of speakers to adapt in language towards the grammatical profiles of their interlocutors) is a well-documented feature of human dialogue widely considered to operate below conscious awareness. Whether large language models exhibit analogous syntactic convergence toward human users relative to human baselines and across a broad range of syntactic constructions remains an open question. Using substitution-paradigm data in which model generations replace one speaker's turns in pre-existing human dialogues, this study measures turn-adjacent reuse of context-free grammar (CFG) rules across sixteen open-weight Llama and Gemma models (1B-70B, pretrained and instruction-tuned) at 1,901 matched positions per model. Every model showed greater CFG-rule overlap with the preceding human turn than with a sampled unrelated human prime, and in every model this actual-versus-random difference was larger for lower-frequency rules. Each instruction-tuned model also showed greater natural-output overlap with the actual prime than the human response it replaced, and all eight matched architecture pairs exhibited greater actual-prime overlap after instruction tuning. However, relative to pretrained variants, instruction-tuned outputs overlapped more with unrelated primes, showed a smaller actual-versus-random increment, and had lower conditional rule-reuse odds once target rule-set size was held constant. In exploratory analyses, each model exhibited greater mean lexical and semantic similarity to the preceding turn than the matched human responses did. Instruction-tuned models additionally produced responses with greater mean semantic similarity than their pretrained counterparts in all eight architecture pairs, whereas the lexical similarity results were more heterogeneous.

</details>


### [37] [UniMem: Complementary Episodic-to-Parametric Memory for Boundary-Agnostic Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26017)
*Siyu Xia,Chenheng Zhang,Yanting Wu,Haoxuan Li,Jiajun Chai,Xiaohan Wang,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Zhouchen Lin,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniMem是一种自路由框架，通过可学习的路由令牌协调情景缓冲区和可扩展参数化内存，解决LLM代理在无边界任务流中的稳定性-可塑性困境，实现按需扩展内存并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM代理在真实部署中面临的稳定性-可塑性困境：外部检索内存虽能快速吸收新证据但难以内化模式且有推理开销，参数化内存虽稳定高效但依赖显式任务边界和固定参数预算。受人脑启发，需一种自主内存管理框架平衡两者。

Method: 提出UniMem自路由框架，使用可学习路由令牌作为内存控制器，协调情景缓冲区（用于新颖/稀疏任务的检索增强执行）和可扩展参数化内存（用于反复/可靠模式的巩固）。通过解耦任务识别与执行，实现无需任务标签的按需内存扩展。

Result: 在长时程流式任务序列实验中，UniMem持续优于基线，保持执行保真度，在三种骨干模型上平均提升4.0 EM点。

Conclusion: UniMem通过仿脑的互补内存路径和自适应路由，有效解决了无边界任务流中的稳定性-可塑性困境，实现了高效、可扩展的自主内存管理，显著提升了LLM代理在持续学习场景下的性能。

Abstract: Memory is essential for LLM agents to accumulate task experience and reuse task-specific execution strategies. However, real-world deployment over boundary-agnostic and evolving task streams exposes a fundamental stability-plasticity dilemma. External retrieval-based memory can rapidly absorb new evidence, but it often fails to internalize recurring execution patterns and incurs inference-time retrieval overhead. Parametric memory enables stable and efficient execution once learned, but typically relies on explicit task boundaries and fixed parameter budgets. Inspired by the human brain, which balances plasticity and stability through complementary episodic storage and gradual consolidation, we propose UniMem, a self-routing framework for autonomous memory management. UniMem uses learnable routing tokens as memory controllers, enabling adaptive coordination between complementary memory pathways: novel or sparse tasks are retained in an episodic buffer for retrieval-augmented execution, while recurring and reliable patterns are consolidated into expandable parametric memory. By decoupling task identification from task execution with routing tokens and parametric memory blocks, UniMem expands memory on demand without task labels during deployment or uncontrolled parameter growth. Experiments on long-horizon streaming task sequences show that UniMem consistently outperforms baselines while maintaining execution fidelity, achieving an average gain of 4.0 EM points across three backbone models.

</details>


### [38] [Pass the Baton: Trajectory-Relayed On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26057)
*Haolei Xu,Xiaowen Xu,Haiwen Hong,Zixuan Ni,Hongxing Li,Yiwen Qiu,Weiming Lu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Relay On-Policy Distillation (Relay-OPD)，通过利用教师-学生在失败前缀上的续写不对称性（教师倾向纠偏、学生倾向延续错误），以无标签触发器让教师在关键早期位置短暂接管生成“教师段”，再交还学生继续训练。在八个数学推理基准上，Relay-OPD 全部取得最优或次优结果，1.7B 模型平均比标准 OPD 提升 5.73%、比最强基线 FastOPD 提升 1.49%，0.6B 模型也有稳定增益，且训练轨迹长度减少 50% 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 On-Policy Distillation (OPD) 存在前缀失效问题：学生一旦在推理早期偏离正确方向，后续生成全建立在错误前缀上，导致监督信号不可靠且浪费算力。

Method: 1. 发现教师-学生在失败前缀上的续写不对称性：教师倾向重定向纠错，学生倾向沿原错误方向继续。2. 将该不对称性转化为无标签的切换触发器。3. 训练时，在触发点让教师短暂接管生成“教师段”，随后学生接管并在此混合轨迹上优化。4. 设置有限的 Relay 预算，将干预集中在关键早期位置，同时限制偏离学生策略。

Result: 以 Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 为教师、Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B-Non-Thinking 为学生，在八个数学推理基准上 Relay-OPD 均获最优或次优。1.7B 模型平均较标准 OPD +5.73%、较 FastOPD +1.49%；0.6B 模型亦有持续提升。训练轨迹长度缩减 50% 以上。

Conclusion: Relay-OPD 通过教师在关键早期步骤的短暂接管，有效解决了 OPD 的前缀失效问题，在大幅缩短训练轨迹的同时显著提升了小模型的数学推理能力，是一种高效且免标签的蒸馏新范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) grounds token-level supervision in the student's own trajectory, yet suffers from prefix failure: once the student commits to a wrong reasoning direction, all subsequent generation builds on this deviation, producing misdirected continuations that elicit unreliable supervision and waste compute. We identify a teacher-student continuation asymmetry on failed prefixes, where the teacher tends to redirect while the student continues along the original direction, and convert it into a label-free handoff trigger in Relay On-Policy Distillation (Relay-OPD). During training, Relay-OPD constructs relay trajectories by letting the teacher briefly take over at detected trigger points to produce a teacher leg, after which the student resumes and is optimized on the resulting trajectory. A limited relay budget concentrates intervention on critical early positions while limiting departure from the student policy. With a Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 teacher and Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B-Non-Thinking students on eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, Relay-OPD achieves the best or second-best results on every benchmark, outperforming standard OPD by +5.73% and the strongest baseline FastOPD by +1.49% on average for 1.7B, with consistent gains at 0.6B. Training trajectory length is reduced by over 50%.

</details>


### [39] [Measuring and Improving Behavioral Consistency in Large Language Models through Fact-Heuristic-Emotion State Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24765)
*Gi-Hun Lee,Joong Yull Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出认知核心模型(CKM)，一种提示词级别的状态强制层，强制大语言模型在决策前将输入分离为事实、启发式、情感三种认知角色，无需修改模型权重即可显著降低输出变异性和决策翻转率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在同一决策问题上多次运行会给出不同答案，且在自身先前答案作为上下文时会反转决策。作者探究这种不稳定性是否可度量且可在不改变模型权重的情况下部分缓解。

Method: CKM 引入结构化状态 S_t = {F_t, H_t, E_t} 并通过转移函数更新，要求模型在决策前将输入分类为事实(给定或可验证)、启发式(推断或假设)、情感(评价或优先级信号)。在韩语决策场景(歧义、伦理冲突、资源分配、错误处理)上评估 26 个来自四家厂商的 LLM，共 37,403 次观测，包含四项核心实验、四臂消融、五臂假消融及温度探针。

Result: (1) CKM 显著降低重复输出变异性 (随机效应 Hedges' g=1.09, 95% CI [0.83,1.35], 31 模型对)；(2) 状态持久化使新型模型决策翻转率下降 82% (g=1.52)；(3) 效果非仅源于 JSON 格式 (仅数值重算 g=2.24)；(4) 固定锚定状态下内在随机性可忽略；(5) 采样随机性下优势扩大 (温度 0.7 时 g=2.87)；(6) 假消融显示约 45% 收益来自结构脚手架，55% 来自事实/启发式/情感内容，CKM 是唯一同时提升一致性并降低翻转的实验臂。CKM 不提升推理正确性。

Conclusion: 行为一致性可度量、因模型而异，且可通过强制模型在决策前分离事实、假设与评价信号来部分改善。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can give different answers to the same decision problem across runs, and reverse a decision when their own prior answer returns as context. We ask whether this instability can be measured and partially reduced without changing model weights.
  We test the Cognitive Kernel Model (CKM), a prompt-level state-enforcement layer. Before deciding, the model must separate its input into three epistemic roles: Fact (given or verifiable), Heuristic (inferred or assumed), and Emotion (evaluative or priority signal). CKM adds no capability; it forces the model to track what kind of information it uses before acting. Formally it maintains a structured state S_t = {F_t, H_t, E_t} updated by a transition function.
  We evaluate CKM on Korean-language decision scenarios (ambiguity, ethical conflict, resource allocation, error handling) across 26 LLMs from four vendors and 37,403 observations, via four core experiments, a 4-arm ablation, a 5-arm sham-restriction ablation, and a temperature probe. Findings:
  (1) CKM reduces repeated-output variability (random-effects Hedges' g=1.09, 95% CI [0.83, 1.35], 31 model pairs);
  (2) state persistence cuts the decision-flip rate by 82% in newer models (g=1.52);
  (3) the effect is not JSON formatting alone (value-only recomputation, g=2.24);
  (4) intrinsic randomness under fixed anchor states is negligible;
  (5) the advantage grows under sampling stochasticity (g=2.87 at temperature 0.7);
  (6) a sham ablation attributes about 45% of the gain to structural scaffolding and 55% to Fact/Heuristic/Emotion content, and CKM is the only arm that both raises consistency and reduces flipping.
  CKM does not improve reasoning correctness. The narrower result: behavioral consistency is measurable, varies across models, and is partially improvable by forcing models to separate facts, assumptions, and evaluative signals before deciding.

</details>


### [40] [CogArena: A Multimethod Evaluation of Cognitive Ability Structure in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24999)
*Dengzhe Hou,Lingyu Jiang,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori D Yamada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 CogArena 基准，系统评估大语言模型（LLM）的认知能力画像是否稳定且可推广。实验表明：虽然任务间相关性普遍为正且存在主成分，但五维理论分组的内聚优势微弱、对评分敏感且跨模型家族不稳定；针对性脚手架干预虽显示小幅匹配分组优势，但未通过多重比较校正，且不能提升对保留模型家族的预测。冻结验证标准失效，替代措辞复实验同样失败。结论为：现有证据不足以确立稳定的五维认知画像，CogArena 提供了一个在贴标签前整合行为特征、协方差、匹配干预和跨家族预测的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 认知评分常被汇总为各能力维度的画像，但这些维度是否能跨任务收敛、对匹配干预表现出选择性响应、以及推广到定义它们之外的模型，尚未得到严格验证。论文旨在建立一个多方法框架，判断何时认知任务得分值得贴上维度标签。

Method: 构建 CogArena：一个程序化生成的 13 范式基准，围绕五个理论动机的分组设计。在 55 个开放权重模型上测量范式相关性、主成分方差解释、分组内聚优势。另进行冻结的、完全交叉的研究：12 个模型来自 6 个家族，施加针对性脚手架干预，检验匹配分组优势、多重比较校正后的脚手架特异性对比、选择性对保留家族的预测增益。并进行事后替代措辞复现。

Result: 55 模型中几乎所有范式相关为正，公共轴解释约一半方差。分组内聚优势小、依赖评分方式、跨家族不确定。冻结研究中针对性脚手架显示小幅匹配分组优势，但无脚手架特异性对比经多重校正，选择性不提升保留家族预测。冻结验证标准失效，替代措辞复现估计量更小且失败。理论对齐提示仅产生电池内微弱对角倾向，不足以确立稳定五维画像。

Conclusion: 证据支持一个边界结论：理论对齐提示在测试电池内呈现微弱的对角倾向，但当前证据无法确立稳定的五维认知画像。CogArena 提供了一个在给模型得分贴认知标签前，整合行为特征、协方差结构、匹配干预和跨家族预测的工作流。

Abstract: LLM cognitive scores are increasingly summarized as per-ability profiles whose dimensions should converge across tasks, respond selectively to matched interventions, and generalize beyond the models used to define them. We introduce CogArena, a procedurally generated 13-paradigm benchmark built around a multimethod framework for determining when cognitive-task scores warrant dimensional labels across five theory-motivated groupings. Across 55 open-weight models, nearly all paradigm correlations are positive and a common axis explains about half the variance. The within-grouping advantage is small, scoring-sensitive, and uncertain across model families. In a separately frozen, fully crossed study across 12 models from six families, targeted scaffolds show a small matched-grouping advantage, but no scaffold-specific contrast survives multiplicity correction and selectivity does not improve held-out-family prediction. The frozen confirmation criterion fails. A post-hoc alternate-wording replication produces a smaller positive estimate and again fails. Together, these results support a boundary conclusion. Theory-aligned prompting produces a small in-battery diagonal tendency, but the present evidence does not establish stable five-dimensional profiles. CogArena provides a workflow joining behavioral signatures, covariance, matched interventions, and out-of-family prediction before cognitive labels are attached to model scores.

</details>

### [41] [Mechanisms of Width Scaling in Normalized Residual Networks: The Effective Alignment Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24887)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Guangming Tan,Fangming Liu,Daning Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有效对齐维度，用于量化有限样本下残差扩展方向在测试数据上的泛化能力，给出不对齐概率的有限样本上界，并通过实验验证更宽模型具有更大的有效对齐维度和更低的不对齐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络宽度理论主要关注渐近极限，缺乏对有限训练数据识别的扩展方向在未见数据上是否仍然有益的指导。

Method: 引入有效对齐维度描述激活梯度的信噪几何，推导独立估计的训练和测试梯度内积的精确均值和方差，获得不对齐概率的有限样本上界，仅依赖有效对齐维度和有效样本量，无需协方差谱假设或规定宽度增长率；将该证书集成到训练-测试残差扩展框架中，得到测试风险改进的高概率条件。

Result: 在宽度可控的LLaMA风格Transformers、Pythia和ResNet-20上实验表明，更宽模型表现出更大的有效对齐维度和更低的经验不对齐；直接残差干预证实对齐统计量能预测保留损失变化的符号和幅度。

Conclusion: 有效对齐维度为有限样本下的残差扩展提供了可测量的泛化保证，无需强假设，实证验证了其在实际模型中的预测能力。

Abstract: Existing theories of neural-network width characterize asymptotic limits, but provide limited guidance on whether an expansion direction identified from finite training data remains beneficial on unseen data. We study this problem for function-preserving residual expansion and introduce the effective alignment dimension, a measurable quantity describing the signal-noise geometry of activation gradients. By deriving the exact mean and variance of the inner product between independently estimated training and test gradients, we obtain a finite-sample upper bound on misalignment probability. The bound depends only on the effective alignment dimension and an effective sample size, requiring finite second moments and a nonzero population gradient, without covariance spectral assumptions or prescribed width-growth rates. We integrate this certificate into the train-test residual-expansion framework, yielding a high-probability condition for test-risk improvement. Experiments across width-controlled LLaMA-style Transformers, Pythia, and ResNet-20 show that wider models exhibit larger effective alignment dimensions and lower empirical misalignment. Direct residual interventions confirm that the alignment statistic predicts the sign and magnitude of held-out loss changes.

</details>


### [42] [GAUGE: Grading Agent-Built Financial Models Without a Golden Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24889)
*Jiacheng Lu,Sinuo Wang,Wentao Zhao,Rui Sun,Cheng Hua,Tao Song,Hui Cai,Beidi Luan,Zhengze Wu,Lingjing Teng,Yijia He,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAUGE 基准测试通过对比真实分析师实践而非单一标准答案来评估智能体构建的估值模型，结果显示当前最佳智能体得分 53.4，优于金融学生（43.2）但落后于所有资深分析师（88.3）及大多数初级分析师（66.0），智能体在模型构建（机械性指标通过率 93%）远强于估值判断（判断性指标通过率 78%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准倾向于用单一专家参考答案评分财务模型输出（预测、折现率、目标价），但专业分析师间存在显著分歧：108 对同期模型中位数单参考得分仅 0.33，92.6% 得分低于 0.70，无一组隐含价格在 10% 内一致。单一参考评分会惩罚专业人士间合理的分歧。

Method: 构建 GAUGE 基准：收集 1,001 份供应商分类的分析师工作簿，设计 196 任务评估集；采用三层观测实践包络、56 个可审计侧面、8 个有效性门控及确定性结构检查；通过 55 人已知群体研究、公司分组交叉拟合及评委稳定性审计进行验证。

Result: 在失效感知得分 φ₀ 上，资深分析师均值 88.3，初级分析师 66.0，金融学生 43.2；24 个智能体中最佳得分 53.4，超过学生均值但低于所有资深及多数初级分析师。智能体通过 93% 机械性侧面、78% 判断性侧面，车队中位数差距 26 分。

Conclusion: 当前智能体在模型构建能力上显著强于估值判断能力。研究发布了方法论、分级去标识化数据层、受控训练分割、版本化 48 任务评估核心及预留刷新池。

Abstract: Financial models combine public disclosures with analyst assumptions to produce forecasts and valuations. While some components can be checked mechanically, forecasts, discount rates, and target prices often admit multiple reasonable answers. Existing benchmarks nevertheless tend to grade such outputs against a single expert reference. Using independently built analyst models for the same companies, we find that across 108 directed pairs covering 65 companies, the median single-reference score is 0.33, 92.6% score below 0.70, and no same-vintage pair agrees on implied price within 10%. Point-tolerance grading can therefore penalize disagreement already present among professionals. We introduce GAUGE, a benchmark for evaluating agent-built valuation models against observed analyst practice rather than a single point answer. GAUGE uses 1,001 vendor-classified analyst workbooks and a 196-task evaluation set, with a three-layer observed-practice envelope, 56 auditable facets, eight validity gates, and deterministic structural checks. We validate the benchmark with a 55-participant known-groups study, company-grouped cross-fitting, and judge-stability audits. On the failure-aware score $φ_0$, senior analysts average 88.3, juniors 66.0, and finance students 43.2. Across 24 agents and 1,011 scored generations, the best agent scores 53.4, above the student mean but below every senior and most juniors. It passes 93% of mechanical facets and 78% of judgment facets, with a fleet-median gap of 26 points. Current agents are substantially stronger at model construction than valuation judgment. We release the methodology, a gated de-identified data tier, a controlled training split, a versioned 48-task evaluation core, and a withheld refresh pool.

</details>


### [43] [LLM as Forecasting Planner: Training-Free Text Conditioning for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24892)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dai Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出LAFP框架，利用LLM作为规划器在TSFM生成的预测轨迹上进行蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），无需重新训练即可实现文本条件时间序列预测，并在多个基准上持续提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件时间序列预测需要同时具备可靠的数值预测能力（TSFM）和语境推理能力（LLM），但直接让LLM生成或修正预测值会破坏TSFM捕获的时间结构。

Method: 将预测建模为在TSFM生成轨迹上的规划问题：冻结的TSFM作为模拟器提出数值延续，LLM作为策略（Ranker）和价值函数（Judge）指导候选选择和轨迹评估。实例化为LAFP，采用MCTS在预测 horizon 上搜索，无需训练即可弥合模态鸿沟。

Result: 在Context-is-Key和Time-MMD基准上，使用Chronos和TimesFM两种TSFM主干及四种LLM的实验表明，LAFP在不同模型选择下均带来一致性提升，验证了序列搜索作为免训练文本条件预测的有效性。

Conclusion: LAFP通过MCTS在TSFM轨迹上引入LLM作为规划器，成功融合数值预测与语境推理，提供了一种免训练、模型无关的文本条件时间序列预测范式。

Abstract: Text-conditioned time-series forecasting predicts a series from both its numerical history and natural-language context, allowing forecasts to account for events and constraints that the past alone cannot reveal. This requires both reliable numerical forecasting and the ability to interpret contextual information. Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide strong numerical forecasts, while large language models (LLMs) can reason over text, but combining their strengths remains challenging because asking an LLM to generate or revise forecast values directly can distort the temporal structure captured by the TSFM. We instead formulate forecasting as a planning problem over TSFM-generated trajectories. The frozen TSFM acts as a simulator that proposes numerical continuations, while the LLM acts as a policy and value function that guides candidate selection and evaluates completed trajectories against the context. We instantiate this as \rc{} (\textbf{L}LM \textbf{A}s \textbf{F}orecasting \textbf{P}lanner), a training-free framework that bridges the modality gap without retraining either model, using Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) over the forecast horizon with a \emph{Ranker} LLM as policy and a \emph{Judge} LLM as value function. Experiments on Context-is-Key and Time-MMD across two TSFM backbones (Chronos and TimesFM) and four LLMs show that \rc{} delivers consistent improvements across model choices, supporting sequential search as an effective training-free approach to text-conditioned forecasting.

</details>


### [44] [Inverse RL Helps Align AI by Imitating Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24900)
*Michał Wiliński,Liu Leqi,Chirag Nagpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PARED（基于演示的投影对齐奖励估计），一种从专家演示中恢复隐式奖励为显式函数的方法，通过轻量级判别器在响应级特征空间分离演示与策略样本，无需任务特定偏好标注，支持推理时重排序、对抗性在线策略强化学习及语境对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法主要依赖监督微调或基于验证器/人类反馈的强化学习，留下了一个核心问题未充分探索：演示本身能否提供一种可检视、可复用、可在线策略优化的隐式奖励？本文受逆强化学习启发，旨在解决这一问题。

Method: PARED 将专家演示背后的隐式奖励建模为少量响应级特征上的显式函数，训练一个轻量级判别器在该特征空间中区分演示与策略自身生成的样本。演示提供任务特定监督，可结合 AI 反馈作为额外监督维度。无需任务特定偏好标注。

Result: 实验表明，恢复的奖励在无监督损失下即可提升基础策略，在标准监督微调后进一步优化可获得额外增益。同时展示了 PARED 可用于语境对齐，即单一策略可针对不同受众偏好进行定制。

Conclusion: PARED 实现了从演示中提取可显式优化的奖励函数，消除了对偏好标注的依赖，支持在线策略优化与多语境对齐，为语言模型对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Language model alignment aims to make model behavior reliably reflect desirable properties such as helpfulness, safety, and instruction following. Current approaches typically use supervised fine-tuning on demonstrations or reinforcement learning with rewards derived from verifiers or human feedback. These paradigms leave an important question underexplored: can demonstrations alone yield an implicit reward that can be inspected, reused, and optimized on-policy to align AI? Motivated by inverse reinforcement learning, we introduce Projected Alignment Reward Estimated from Demonstrations (PARED). PARED recovers the implicit reward underlying expert demonstrations as an explicit function over a small set of response-level features, learned by a lightweight discriminator that separates demonstrations from the policy's own samples in this feature space. Unlike a standard reward model, PARED requires no task-specific preference annotations: demonstrations provide the task-specific supervision, which can be augmented with AI feedback as additional dimensions of supervision. Through experiments involving inference-time reranking and adversarial on-policy RL, we show that the recovered reward improves a base policy without a supervised loss and yields further gains when optimized after standard supervised fine-tuning. Additionally, we demonstrate that PARED can be used for contextual alignment, in which a single policy can be tailored to the preferences of different audiences.

</details>


### [45] [Multiclass Classification without Labels via Posterior Simplex Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24943)
*Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini,Johann Ioannou-Nikolaides,Troels Petersen,Vincenzo Piuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将CWoLa原理推广至多类别场景（K>2），证明最优混合分类器将数据映射为(K-1)-单纯形，提出基于单纯形拟合或瓶颈架构的先验自由方法，仅用混合身份标签即可恢复潜在类别及其比例，在MNIST、CIFAR-10、Galaxy10上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际分类问题中可靠的实例级标签不可获取，但可通过不同切分、来源、群体或实验条件构建弱增强的无标签混合样本。现有CWoLa方法仅适用于二分类（K=2），缺乏多类别（K>2）的理论推广和实用算法。

Method: 1) 理论证明：多类别混合模型下，贝叶斯最优混合分类器将数据映射到混合后验空间中的(K-1)-单纯形，单纯形顶点由未知混合矩阵诱导的潜在类别决定。2) 算法：先训练标准分类器区分混合身份，再通过后处理单纯形拟合或瓶颈架构提取潜在类别结构，无需先验知识。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、Galaxy10 DECaLS数据集上实验表明，仅利用混合身份标签即可恢复潜在类别及其在混合中的比例，性能接近全监督基线，显著缩小了弱监督与全监督的性能差距。

Conclusion: 论文为多类别无标签混合学习提供了数学严谨、可扩展的工具，证明了仅凭混合身份即可实现潜在类别发现和比例估计，为标签稀缺领域的多类别发现奠定了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: In many classification problems, reliable instance-level labels are unavailable. However, it is often possible to construct weakly enriched unlabeled samples: datasets selected by different cuts, sources, populations, or experimental conditions that change latent class proportions without revealing them. Classification without Labels (CWoLa) shows that, in the binary case ($K=2$), a classifier trained to distinguish two impure mixtures with different class proportions can recover an optimal class discriminator without knowing the mixture proportions. We extend this principle to multiclass learning from several unlabeled mixtures ($K>2$), where the learner observes only mixture identity and neither latent class labels nor class-prior matrices. We prove that, for a multiclass mixture model, the Bayes-optimal mixture classifier $g^\star$ maps data points into a $(K-1)$-simplex embedded in mixture-posterior space. The $K$ vertices of this simplex are induced by the latent classes through the unknown mixing matrix. Leveraging this geometry, we propose prior-free procedures that train a standard classifier to distinguish mixture identities and then extract latent class structure using either post-hoc simplex fitting or a bottleneck architecture. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Galaxy10 DECaLS show that mixture identity alone can recover latent classes and their fractions in the mixture. By narrowing the gap between weakly supervised and fully supervised performance, we provide a mathematically grounded, scalable tool for multiclass discovery in label-scarce domains.

</details>

*Mehdi Rahimifar,Amin Darabi,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Xing Huang,Yao Wang,Zhijun Tu,Yufei Cui,Yunke Peng,Hongliang Li*
