Notable observations include evidence that model non-determinism produces inconsistent security outcomes and that autonomous agent behavior can cross system boundaries in ways that OS sandboxing directly mitigates. Initial characteristics toward a control harness fitness framework are proposed, and a third research question is identified for future investigation.

</details>


### [215] [Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer,Guillaume Allegre,Tony O'Halloran,Alexandre Sallinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Messier，一个统一的 AI 代理评估语料库，包含 957,253 条记录，涵盖 30 个基准、714 个代理、11,891 个任务和 74,205 个验证器。通过标准化记录并补充六个专业领域的实验，研究发现前沿进展不均衡：函数调用已饱和、编程进步最快、企业工作流最具挑战。反事实重评分显示严格全通过聚合规则会掩盖进展并改变排名。由此派生的能力量表与 Epoch 的评估能力指数高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.81），并可按领域、职业、动作空间或验证器类型专门化。Messier 为代理能力缩放、基准审计和评估失败细粒度分析提供了可复用基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 代理交互环境评估面临任务、脚手架、验证器和评分规则碎片化问题，现有工作局限于狭窄设置、规模有限或需要昂贵重跑，导致实证记录难以比较。需要统一、大规模、标准化的语料库来支持跨基准、跨领域的能力分析和基准审计。

Method: 构建 Messier 语料库：整合公开基准分数，补充五代理在六个代表性不足的专业和科学领域（含法律基准）的运行记录。每条记录按模型、脚手架、环境、任务、验证器、聚合规则标准化，并附加 SOC/NAICS 职业和行业分类。使用该语料库进行前沿进展分析、反事实重评分实验，并推导能力量表与 Epoch Evaluation Capability Index 对比验证。

Result: 发现前沿进展不均衡：函数调用任务已饱和，编程任务进步最快，企业工作流任务最具挑战。反事实重评分表明，多验证器任务中严格的全通过聚合规则会掩盖进展并人为改变代理排名。推导的能力量表与 Epoch Evaluation Capability Index 排名高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.81），且可按领域、职业、动作空间或验证器类型专门化。

Conclusion: Messier 提供了一个基础的、可复用的基础设施，用于 AI 代理能力缩放、基准审计和评估失败的细粒度分析。标准化的大规模语料库使跨基准、跨领域的可比较评估成为可能，揭示了聚合规则对排名的影响，并为专门化能力量表提供了数据支持。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents in interactive environments is hindered by fragmented tasks, scaffolds, verifiers, and scoring rules. Existing efforts focus on narrow settings, remain limited in scale, or require costly reruns, leaving much of the empirical record incomparable. We introduce Messier, a unified corpus of 957,253 records that span 30 benchmarks, 714 agents, 11,891 tasks, and 74,205 verifiers. Messier consolidates public benchmark scores and supplements them with five-agent runs across six underrepresented professional and scientific domains, including a recent legal benchmark. Each record is standardized by model, scaffold, environment, task, verifier, and aggregation rule, with SOC/NAICS classifications for occupational and industry analysis. Using this corpus, we show frontier progress is uneven across benchmark types, with "function calling" saturated, "programming" improving the fastest, and "enterprise workflows" remaining the most challenging. Furthermore, counterfactual rescoring shows that strict all-pass aggregation in multi-verifier tasks can obscure progress and artificially alter agent rankings. From these standardized records, we derive capability scales that align with Epoch's Evaluation Capability Index rankings at Spearman \r{ho} = 0.81 and can be specialized by domain, occupation, action space, or verifier type. Messier provides a foundational, reusable infrastructure for agent capability scaling, benchmark auditing, and fine-grained analysis of evaluation failures.

</details>


### [216] [Interactive Reward Agent: GUI Task Evaluation via Environment-State Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25904)
*Chenrui Shi,Yuwei Wu,Yang Liu,Ruining Feng,Zirui Shang,Zhi Gao,Lifeng Fan,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出交互式奖励代理（IRA），基于“先提出再验证”的框架，通过调用系统工具、应用工具和GUI工具从执行后环境获取并验证证据，结合可见界面与环境状态实现可靠的GUI任务评估。并构建了GUI-RewardBench基准，实验显示IRA准确率达86.9%，在GUI智能体强化学习中取得34.0% OSWorld成功率，证明其能提供有效奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化GUI任务评估因判断往往需要访问系统配置、文件数据、应用设置等环境状态（超出屏幕截图），仍具挑战性。现有评估器难以可靠获取并验证这些隐性证据，限制了其作为测试时扩展和后训练奖励信号的效果。

Method: 提出交互式奖励代理（IRA），采用提出-验证框架：给定任务指令与执行后GUI环境，IRA首先生成任务完成条件，再通过调用系统工具、应用工具、GUI工具交互式验证条件，融合可见界面与环境状态证据。同时发布GUI-RewardBench基准，包含321条跨10类Ubuntu桌面应用的任务轨迹。

Result: 在GUI-RewardBench上，IRA准确率达86.9%，显著优于现有评估器基线。将IRA作为奖励信号用于GUI智能体强化学习，在OSWorld基准上取得34.0%成功率，验证其训练有效性。

Conclusion: IRA通过交互式环境证据获取与验证，解决了GUI任务评估依赖隐性状态的难题，提供高准确度的自动化评估，并能作为强化学习的有效奖励信号显著提升GUI智能体性能。

Abstract: Graphical user interface task evaluation aims to determine whether a GUI agent has successfully completed a user instruction. Automated GUI task evaluation has received increasing attention because the evaluation results can serve as reward signals for both test-time scaling and post-training. However, reliable GUI task evaluation remains challenging because the judgments often require access to environment states, such as system configurations, file data, and application settings, beyond the screenshots of execution trajectories. In this paper, we propose an interactive reward agent (IRA) based on a propose-then-verify framework to acquire and verify evidence from the post-execution environment. Given a task instruction and a GUI environment after the GUI agent execution, IRA first proposes the task completion conditions and then verifies them by invoking system tools, application tools, and GUI tools. This design combines evidence from both visible interfaces and the environment state in an interactive process. We further introduce GUI-RewardBench, a benchmark of 321 GUI task trajectories spanning 10 Ubuntu desktop application categories. Experiments show that IRA achieves 86.9% accuracy on GUI-RewardBench, outperforming existing evaluator baselines. We further apply IRA to reinforcement learning of GUI agents, achieving a 34.0% OSWorld success rate, which demonstrates that IRA can provide effective reward signals for training GUI agents.

</details>


### [217] [Toward Standardized Cross-Vendor Agent Tool Trust Management in Autonomous Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25914)
*Ravi Kant Sharma,Ashutosh Uttam,Ajay Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentToolMO，一种3GPP NRM信息模型，用于跨厂商AI代理工具信任管理，通过标准化信任状态机、阻尼级联传播、跨厂商信任通知和依赖图回溯评估，实现近实时故障遏制与有界收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网络L4/L5级要求AI代理跨厂商无人工干预调用工具，但现有管理标准缺乏跨厂商信任可见性机制；当厂商B工具被攻陷时，厂商A代理仍继续调用，导致级联服务故障。

Method: 设计AgentToolMO模型，包含：1) 形式化定义的信任状态机及可证明的渐进式执行；2) 阻尼级联传播机制保证有界收敛；3) 复用现有管理服务接口实现跨厂商信任通知；4) 基于NRM依赖图遍历的事后影响评估。

Result: 仿真评估显示：标准化跨厂商通知将爆炸半径从小时级未检测传播降至近实时遏制（受限于MnS通知投递延迟），级联收敛在有界迭代内完成，跨厂商域通知开销呈亚线性增长。

Conclusion: 框架完全兼容现有3GPP管理基础设施与协议，为可信多厂商自主网络管理提供了标准化路径，解决了跨厂商工具信任可见性缺失导致的级联故障风险。

Abstract: Autonomous Network Levels 4-5 require AI agents to invoke tools across vendor boundaries without human oversight, yet existing management standards lack a standardized mechanism for cross-vendor trust visibility. When a tool from Vendor B is compromised, agents from Vendor A continue invoking it -- unaware of the trust degradation -- causing cascading service impact. We present AgentToolMO, a proposed 3GPP NRM information model for agent tool trust management. The model comprises: a formally defined trust state machine with provable graduated enforcement, damped cascade propagation with bounded convergence, cross-vendor trust notifications via existing Management Services (MnS) interfaces, and retroactive impact assessment through NRM dependency graph traversal. Simulation-based evaluation across multi-vendor topologies shows that standardized cross-vendor notifications reduce blast radius from hours-scale undetected propagation to near-real-time containment bounded by MnS notification delivery, with cascade convergence guaranteed in bounded iterations and sub-linear notification scaling across vendor domains. The framework operates within existing 3GPP management infrastructure, leverages existing protocols, and provides a standardization pathway for trustworthy multi-vendor autonomous network management.

</details>


### [218] [Penelope: Localized Latent Recurrence for Efficient Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25915)
*Yutong Chen,Shouqian Shi,Xinran Liu,Haochen Wang,Jiaying Wang,Tianxing Xu,Yuanxi Wang,Zirui Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Penelope 是一种高效的潜在推理框架，将循环计算局限于解码器的特定区间，通过渐进式 CoT-to-latent 课程将显式推理转移到内部循环路径，在保持准确率的同时降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂结构化推理任务需要额外计算，现有方法要么增加参数规模（训练部署成本高），要么通过思维链（CoT）序列化中间步骤（将推理计算绑定到自回归输出长度），均存在效率问题。

Method: Penelope 在预训练的仅解码器 Transformer 中引入潜在推理框架，将循环计算局限于选定的解码器区间。下层解码器前缀仅评估一次以构建问题条件边界记忆，随后通过时间调制 GRU 动力学和循环读出状态迭代细化，最后生成答案。采用渐进式 CoT-to-latent 课程将显式推理迁移至内部循环路径。

Result: 在开源结构化推理基准上，Penelope 在验证选定的潜在预算下，达到与现有潜在推理模型具有竞争力的准确率，同时降低了测量的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 潜在细化可以局限于狭窄的解码器区间，减少重复的完整解码器执行，无需生成长可见推理轨迹，为仅解码器 Transformer 模型提供了实用的准确率-效率权衡。

Abstract: Complex structured reasoning tasks often require additional computation, yet current language models obtain it mainly by increasing parameter scale or by serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens. The former raises training and deployment costs, while the latter ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. We introduce Penelope, an efficient latent-reasoning framework for pretrained decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is then iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation to be allocated in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace. Experiments on open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks show that, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency. These results show that latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace and providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

</details>


### [219] [dtControl2+$\varepsilon$: Trading Optimality for Explainability in MDPs via Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25925)
*Tereza Kinská,Jan Křetínský,Tobias Meggendorfer,Sabine Rieder,Maximilian Weininger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于ε最优性的决策树学习方法，扩展了dtControl2工具，允许在可控的精度损失下生成更小、更易理解的决策树策略，实现了对马尔可夫决策过程中控制器的可调节简化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的决策树表示控制器（如dtControl2）虽然具备可解释性，但对于大规模系统或包含许多边界情况的系统，生成的树往往过于复杂，难以被人类理解；直接减小树的大小并不容易，因为遗漏关键案例可能导致控制器错误。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架下，扩展dtControl2引入ε功能：给定允许的不精确度ε ≥ 0，构建更小的决策树，提炼控制器的核心逻辑，同时保证ε-最优性；该方法提供可调节的简化解释，可控地省略细节。

Result: 实验表明，该工具生成的决策树比现有最先进方法小几个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证ε-最优性的前提下，成功生成了显著更小、更易理解的决策树策略，为可解释强化学习提供了可调节的简化机制。

Abstract: Over the past decade, decision trees have been used to represent controllers (a.k.a. policies) in an explainable way, with dtControl2 as a current state-of-the-art tool. However, for systems that are large or have many corner cases, even such representations tend to be too complex and not human-comprehensible. Unfortunately, reducing the size of the decision tree is not straightforward, as missing just a single crucial case might result in an incorrect controller. We tackle this issue in the setting of Markov decision processes, extending dtControl2 by "$\varepsilon$" functionality: Given an allowed imprecision $\varepsilon \geq 0$, we construct a smaller decision tree, distilling the essence of the controller, while still guaranteeing its $\varepsilon$-optimality. This enables us to provide tunably simpler explanations, omitting a controllable amount of detail. Our tool constructs decision trees that are orders of magnitude smaller than the state of the art.

</details>


### [220] [Large Language Model for Operations Research Formulation Selection in Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25956)
*Jintao Xu,Yingzheng Ma,Jiong Dong,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于求解器引导的大语言模型框架，用于多仓库库存分配问题中的混合整数规划公式选择。通过监督微调、边际加权身份偏好优化和组相对策略优化，在京东真实数据上实现了Hit@1从21.45%提升到50.42%，分配准确率比基线提升12.57个百分点，超越固定公式和偏好训练选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多仓库库存分配通常建模为混合整数规划问题，但没有单一公式能持续匹配由需求集中度、库存不平衡、补货规模、服务约束和预测波动性诱导的异构实例级机制。现有方法缺乏针对具体实例的动态公式选择机制。

Method: 提出求解器引导的大语言模型框架：1) 每个OR专家对应一个编码不同分配优先级的MIP公式；2) 构建平衡的专家条件监督微调记录进行模式学习；3) 利用MIP求解器在历史实例上的评估，将求解器评估的分配质量差距转化为边际加权IPO偏好和实例级专家分数元数据；4) 在组相对策略优化中使用奖励查找为采样响应分配奖励。

Result: 在京东商城多仓库库存分配实例上实验：GRPO显著提升专家选择准确率，Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，Hit Ratio@2从70.47%提升至82.31%。最终选择器实现分配准确率比现有基线提升12.57个百分点，优于SFT+IPO选择器和最佳固定OR专家，将与事后神谕的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器引导的LLM框架通过GRPO实现了实例级MIP公式的动态选择，在真实电商场景中显著优于固定公式和传统偏好训练方法，证明了将求解器反馈整合到LLM训练中的有效性，为运筹优化问题中的自动化建模选择提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-warehouse inventory allocation is typically formulated as a mixed-integer programming (MIP) problem, yet no single formulation consistently matches heterogeneous instance-level regimes induced by demand concentration, inventory imbalance, replenishment scale, service constraints, and forecast volatility. We study this issue as instance-wise operations research (OR) formulation selection, where each allocation instance is assigned to a solver-executable formulation from a candidate OR expert library. We propose a solver-guided large language model (LLM) framework for OR formulation selection, in which each OR expert corresponds to a MIP formulation encoding a distinct allocation priority. To train the selector, the framework first constructs balanced expert-conditioned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) records for schema learning, and then uses MIP solver evaluation on historical instances to convert solver-evaluated allocation-quality gaps into margin-weighted identity preference optimization (IPO) preferences and per-instance expert-score metadata for reward lookup during group relative policy optimization (GRPO) to assign rewards to sampled responses. Experiments on multi-warehouse inventory allocation instances from JD$\mathord{.}$com, one of China's largest e-retailers, demonstrate that GRPO substantially improves expert-selection accuracy relative to the SFT+IPO selector and, more importantly, produces higher realized allocation quality than both the preference-trained selector and the best fixed formulation. With GRPO, Hit Ratio@1 and Hit Ratio@2 increase from 21.45% to 50.42% and from 70.47% to 82.31%. The resulting selector achieves an allocation accuracy gain of 12.57 percentage points over the incumbent baseline, outperforming both the SFT+IPO selector and the best fixed OR expert, and reduces the gap to the ex-post oracle to 4.85 percentage points.

</details>


### [221] [CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26023)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Yuxiao Huang,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CHARM，一种用于多模态图零样本迁移的图基础模型，通过层次化上下文建模捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系，将域特定节点模式映射为共享高层概念，减少对目标域监督的依赖，实验在零样本多模态图任务上持续提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的图基础模型通常需要下游适配，而基于LLM的图方法主要处理单模态图或单域任务，多模态图上的零样本迁移仍未充分探索。关键挑战包括：模型需从单个模态泛化知识并捕获可迁移的跨模态关系；在无目标域微调情况下，节点表示与域特定结构和模态特定特征纠缠，遮蔽了未见域中的共享概念。

Method: CHARM采用层次化上下文建模，用层次化图上下文替代孤立原始节点，捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系，将域特定节点模式映射为共享高层概念。设计模态感知图上下文编码器，融合多模态信息与图结构，将生成的表示转换为图令牌供大语言模型使用，实现零样本迁移。

Result: 实验在零样本多模态图任务上显示出持续的性能提升，验证了CHARM在跨域零样本迁移中的有效性。

Conclusion: CHARM通过层次化上下文建模和模态感知编码器，成功解决了多模态图零样本迁移中的跨模态泛化和域特定纠缠问题，为图基础模型在多模态零样本场景下的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for transferring knowledge across graph domains and tasks. Real-world graphs associate nodes with text, images, and other modalities, making multimodal graphs essential for representing complex entities and relations. Moreover, collecting labels and adapting models for every new graph domain is costly and often infeasible, motivating zero-shot transfer. Unfortunately, zero-shot transfer on multimodal graphs remains underexplored. Existing GNN-based graph foundation models typically require downstream adaptation, whereas LLM-based graph methods mainly address unimodal graphs or tasks within a single domain. This setting presents two key challenges. First, models must generalize knowledge from individual modalities while capturing transferable cross-modal relations. Second, without target-domain fine-tuning, node representations remain entangled with domain-specific structures and modality-specific characteristics, obscuring shared concepts in unseen domains. To address these challenges, we propose CHARM, a multimodal graph foundation model with hierarchical context modeling for zero-shot transfer. CHARM replaces isolated raw nodes with hierarchical graph contexts that capture multimodal semantics and cross-modal relations. These contexts map domain-specific node patterns to shared high-level concepts, reducing reliance on target-domain supervision or adaptation. A modality-aware graph context encoder integrates multimodal information with graph structure and converts the resulting representations into graph tokens for a large language model . Experiments show consistent improvements on zero-shot multimodal graph tasks.

</details>


### [222] [Falling Behind Drives Unsafe Development in an Idealised AI Race Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26034)
*Elias Fernández Domingos,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过行为实验和演化模型探讨AI竞赛中不安全开发的成因，发现竞争压力和对手行为比风险偏好更能解释不安全选择，建议政策应减少竞争压力、促进合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术竞赛中速度与安全的张力尤为突出，尤其是AI领域，竞争压力常被认为会激励更冒险、更不注重安全的开发。研究旨在理解这种动态下不安全行为的成因。

Method: 设计了一个基于理想化AI竞赛的框架行为实验，配对参与者在不确定时间范围内反复选择安全或不安全开发。不安全开发提供更快进展和更高即时收益，但积累私人风险，最高风险分三个处理（10%、60%、90%）。竞争结构固定，仅最大风险变化。辅以包含四种策略（始终安全、始终不安全、条件安全、条件反社会安全）的简化演化模型进行理论解释。

Result: 预先注册的风险水平比较和风险偏好的作用未获数据支持。探索性分析显示：不安全选择受竞赛动态战略状态影响大于风险偏好——对手选择不安全后参与者更倾向不安全，领先减少不安全行为，落后增加不安全行为，首轮选择预测后续行为。演化模型复现处理效应，展示条件不安全行为如何在竞争动力学中被青睐。

Conclusion: 不安全开发源于早期行为惯性、对手行为和落后恐惧，而非仅源于风险偏好。政策应聚焦于降低AI开发中的竞争压力、促进合作，而非仅关注个体风险偏好。

Abstract: Technological races create tension between speed and safety: actors may gain by moving faster than competitors, even when risky development is harmful. This is prominent in debates about artificial intelligence (AI), where competitive pressure is often argued to incentivise riskier, less safety-conscious development. We study this using a framed behavioural experiment based on an idealised AI race, in which paired participants repeatedly chose between Safe and Unsafe development under an uncertain time horizon. Unsafe development gave faster progress and higher immediate payoffs but accumulated private risk up to a treatment-specific maximum of 10\%, 60\%, or 90\%; the race's competitive structure was held constant, and only this maximum risk varied. Neither the pre-registered comparison between risk levels nor the role of elicited risk preferences was supported by the data. Instead, exploratory analyses motivated by the task's repeated structure show that Unsafe behaviour is shaped less by risk preferences than by the evolving strategic state of the race: participants are more likely to choose Unsafe after their opponent does so, being ahead reduces Unsafe play while falling behind increases it, and first-round choices predict later behaviour. To interpret these effects we introduce a reduced evolutionary model with four strategies -- Always Safe, Always Unsafe, Conditionally Safe, and Conditionally Antisocial Safe -- which reproduces the treatment effect and shows how conditional Unsafe behaviour can be favoured by competitive race dynamics. Together, the experiment and model show that unsafe development can emerge from early behavioural momentum, opponent behaviour, and fear of falling behind, rather than from risk preferences alone, suggesting policy should focus on reducing competitive pressure and promoting cooperation in AI development rather than only individual risk.

</details>


### [223] [Desktop-Delta Bench: Do Computer-Use Models Understand Desktop GUI Transitions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26041)
*Abhishek Pillai,Samir Kumar Nayak,Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB)，一个用于评估桌面计算机使用代理 (CUA) 步级因果转移重建能力的离线基准，包含 2,013 个人工验证实例，覆盖多应用 Linux 轨迹。实验揭示现有模型在步级推理上存在显著缺口，最佳模型在排序任务上的准确率仅约 65%，且上下文信息虽能提升干扰识别但会降低普通排序准确性。单动作实验显示动作类别识别比定位更难。DDB 填补了 GUI 定位与端到端任务成功之间的诊断空白，有助于针对性改进 CUA 的验证、可靠性和恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要衡量端到端任务成功率或单帧定位，无法单独评估模型重构动作产生的因果任务相关转移的能力，而这对于拒绝陈旧观测、验证进度和从失败中恢复至关重要。难点在于推理、远程输入、应用渲染和截图捕获是异步的：下一观测可能延迟、遮挡、短暂或无关，从而被误读为进度并带入后续规划。

Method: 构建 Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB)：离线步级基准，包含 2,013 个人工验证实例，源自跨约 15 个应用和 50 个任务领域的新颖多应用 Linux 轨迹。针对三大失败维度——状态验证、源追踪、上下文感知控制——设计两类互补任务：463 个三帧时序排序实例（含 105 个跨轨迹干扰项）和 1,550 个前后帧对（标注 5 种动作及其载荷）。评估 8 个闭源和开源模型家族，涵盖 32 种排序设置和 16 种单动作设置。

Result: 观察到一致的性能差距。排序任务远未饱和：最佳非干扰和干扰完全匹配率分别为 65.1% 和 65.7%。任务上下文提升干扰识别 6.9 个百分点，但降低非干扰完全匹配 2.2 个百分点；错误分析显示模型系统性地复制呈现的 A-B-C 顺序。单动作结果表明推断动作家族比定位更难：点击 F1 为 0.96，拖拽为 0.76，但被识别的拖拽通常定位良好。

Conclusion: DDB 通过填补 GUI 定位与最终任务成功之间缺失的诊断层，补充了端到端基准，能够针对性地改进桌面 CUA 的验证、可靠性和恢复能力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly act through desktop GUIs to complete long-horizon tasks. Current benchmarks primarily measure end-task success or single-frame grounding. Neither isolates whether a model can reconstruct the causal, task-relevant transition produced by an action- crucial for rejecting stale observations, verifying progress, and recovering from failure. This is difficult because inference, remote input, app rendering, and screenshot capture are asynchronous: the next observation may be delayed, occluded, transient, or unrelated, then misread as progress and carried into subsequent planning. We introduce Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB), an offline step-level benchmark with 2,013 human-verified instances from novel, multi-app Linux trajectories across ~15 applications and 50 task domains. DDB trajectories targets 3 failure dimensions- state verification, source tracking, and context-aware control- through 2 complementary tasks: 463 3-frame temporal-ordering instances, including 105 with a cross-trajectory decoy, and 1,550 before-after pairs labeled from 5 actions + its payload. We evaluate 8 closed and open-source model families across 32 ordering and 16 single-action settings, observing consistent gaps. Ordering remains unsaturated: best non-decoy and decoy exact-match rates are 65.1% and 65.7%. Task context improves decoy identification by 6.9 percentage points but reduces non-decoy exact match by 2.2 points; error analysis reveals systematic copying of the presented A-B-C order. Single-action results show that inferring the action family is harder than locating it: click F1 is 0.96 vs, 0.76 for drag, while recognized drags are generally localized well. DDB, thus, complements end-to-end benchmarks by filling the missing diagnostic layer between GUI grounding and final task success, enabling targeted improvements to desktop CUA verification, reliability, and recovery.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [CHaystack: Benchmarking Chinese Document Retrieval and VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24760)
*Hanxi Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了 CHaystack，首个面向大规模文档检索与问答的中文 DocumentVQA 基准，涵盖学术论文、广告、网页、实拍文档四大类别；并构建了 CDocRAG 系统，引入 VLM 相关性过滤器校验检索图像。实验显示 Qwen 系列模型在文本密集型文档上显著优于其他模型（Recall@1 71.91 vs 14.40），揭示中文文本编码是核心瓶颈，中文大规模 DocumentVQA 仍有巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DocumentVQA 基准多聚焦单文档或英文场景，缺乏专门针对中文大规模文档检索与问答的综合评测集，难以全面考察系统在多类别、多模态文档上的综合能力。

Method: 1. 构建 CHaystack 基准：收集四大类真实中文文档，设计检索+生成流程的评测任务。2. 设计 CDocRAG 系统：采用 VLM 相关性过滤器对检索到的文档图像进行二次校验，再送入生成模型产出答案。3. 在基准上评测主流开源嵌入模型与生成模型，按文档类别对比检索召回率与生成质量。

Result: Qwen 系列模型在网页、论文等文本密集类别上表现最优，Recall@1 达 71.91%；非 Qwen 模型仅在广告等视觉丰富类别具竞争力，在文本密集类别急剧下降（最佳非 Qwen 仅 14.40%）。检索阶段的中文文本编码能力是决定性瓶颈。

Conclusion: CHaystack 揭示了中文大规模 DocumentVQA 的核心挑战在于中文文本的有效编码与检索，现有开源模型在文本密集型中文文档上仍有显著提升空间，未来需重点强化中文多模态表征学习。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has made substantial progress in extending the memory of large language models (LLMs), and recent advances have further pushed RAG from pure text settings toward multimodal scenarios. In the document understanding domain, document visual question answering (DocumentVQA) has evolved from question answering over a single document to retrieval-and-generation pipelines over large-scale document collections. However, a benchmark specifically designed for Chinese large-scale document retrieval and question answering is still lacking. To bridge this gap, we introduce CHaystack, a new Chinese DocumentVQA benchmark that covers four document categories, namely academic papers, advertisements, web pages, and real-world photographed documents, enabling a more comprehensive evaluation of DocumentVQA systems. In addition, we present CDocRAG, a Chinese DocumentVQA system that uses a VLM-based relevance filter to verify retrieved document images before answer generation. We evaluate representative open-source embedding and generation models on CHaystack. The results reveal a clear contrast in category-wise strengths: Qwen-family models perform best on text-rich documents such as webpages and papers, whereas other models only achieve competitive results on visually rich categories such as advertisements and degrade sharply on text-dense documents. For retrieval, Qwen3-VL reaches 71.91 Recall@1 while the best non-Qwen model achieves only 14.40. These results indicate that the core challenge of CHaystack lies in Chinese textual encoding, and that Chinese large-scale DocumentVQA still leaves substantial room for improvement. Our code and dateset is available at https://github.com/hanxi19/CHaystack.

</details>


### [225] [From Naive RAG to Deep Agentic Retrieval: An Evolving Context Engineering Pipeline for Regulatory Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24791)
*Mishca de Costa,Muhammad Saleh Anwar,Dave Mercier,Issam Hammad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文记录了安大略发电公司(OPG)为满足安大略能源委员会(OEB)监管合规与费率案例分析需求,构建生产级RAG检索管线的演进历程:从朴素RAG、混合检索+重排序、智能体函数调用检索,到具备代码工具合成与显式规划的深度多智能体架构。作者将成熟架构形式化为渐进式证据获取与成本感知升级(PEA-CAE):先低成本高精度检索,仅在预期证据增益足以抵消延迟与成本时才升级至全文阅读。实验表明,对于大规模演进的法规语料,上下文工程比领域特定微调更具可行性与经济性;深度智能体检索的演进呼应经典信息检索思想,引入自适应查询重写、渐进式文档发现、层级子智能体摘要等系统原语,迭代式证据获取与自适应规划正取代单次检索成为企业级问答的基石。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级文档语料规模增长与查询复杂度提升导致朴素RAG触及硬性瓶颈(检索召回率低、上下文窗口溢出、幻觉率高、成本失控),急需一种能在延迟、成本、准确性三者间动态权衡的生产级检索范式,以支撑OPG在OEB监管合规与费率案例分析等高风险场景下的可靠问答。

Method: 采用演进式系统工程方法:依次构建并评估四阶段管线——(1)朴素RAG;(2)混合检索(稀疏+稠密)+重排序;(3)智能体函数调用检索(工具调用式检索);(4)深度多智能体架构(代码工具合成+显式规划)。最终将阶段4形式化为PEA-CAE框架:低成本高精度初检→证据增益预估→条件升级全文读取→层级子智能体摘要聚合。通过真实运营轨迹与消融实验验证各阶段失效模式与权衡。

Result: PEA-CAE在OPG生产语料上显著降低端到端延迟与Token成本,同时保持甚至提升答案准确性;上下文工程(迭代检索、查询重写、分层摘要)在大规模动态法规语料上比领域微调更具可扩展性与性价比;运营日志证实迭代式证据获取与自适应规划已成为企业级问答的主流范式,单次检索逐渐被取代。

Conclusion: 对于大规模、持续演进的企业监管语料,渐进式证据获取与成本感知升级(PEA-CAE)提供了一条比微调更务实、经济的技术路径;深度智能体检索不仅复兴了经典IR的自适应查询重写、渐进式发现、层级摘要等思想,更将其封装为可复用的系统原语,推动企业级RAG从单次检索向搜索式迭代推理范式转型。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is the dominant paradigm for applying large language models (LLMs) to enterprise document corpora, yet naive implementations encounter hard limits as corpus scale and query complexity grow. This paper traces the evolution of a production retrieval pipeline at Ontario Power Generation (OPG) for regulatory compliance and rate case analysis under Ontario Energy Board (OEB) reporting requirements. We examine successive stages: naive RAG, hybrid retrieval with re-ranking, agentic function-calling retrieval, and a deep multi-agent architecture with code-based tool synthesis and explicit planning, and identify the failure modes and tradeoffs that motivated each transition. We formalize the mature architecture as Progressive Evidence Acquisition with Cost-Aware Escalation (PEA-CAE): begin with low-cost, high-precision retrieval and escalate to full-document reads only when the expected evidence gain justifies latency and cost. Our findings show that context engineering is a more tractable and economically viable path than domain-specific fine-tuning for large, evolving regulatory corpora. More broadly, the progression toward deep agentic retrieval mirrors classical information retrieval ideas, introducing adaptive query reformulation, progressive document discovery, and hierarchical subagent summarization as practical system primitives. Operational traces further support the search-based nature of modern retrieval systems, where iterative evidence acquisition and adaptive planning increasingly replace single-pass retrieval as the foundation for enterprise-scale question answering.

</details>


### [226] [AI-Assisted Knowledge Access for Legacy Enterprise Asset Management in Energy Operations: A Practical Retrieval System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24792)
*Dave Mercier,Mishca de Costa,Muhammad Anwar,Mark Randall,Issam Hammad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种检索助手，旨在改善能源公用事业遗留企业资产管理平台上的日常知识获取。通过意图理解、查询重写、混合语义与向量检索、上下文工程、基于事实的答案生成以及确定性超链接转换，结合语义丰富的数据准备流水线，在试点中显著提升了检索质量和用户任务完成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源公用事业仍在长寿命的企业资产管理平台上运行工程工作管理、采购和库存流程。替换这些平台成本高昂且操作破坏性大，因此需要实用的改进层来提升跨供应商文档、运营数据存储模式和用户界面使用三大场景的知识获取效率。

Method: 运行时方法结合意图理解、查询重写、混合语义与向量检索、Token 限制下的上下文工程、基于事实的答案生成，以及面板标识符和引用文档的确定性超链接转换。数据准备流水线以语义丰富为核心质量杠杆，包括添加表和字段描述、跨源规范化首字母缩写、在有用时索引代表性行级上下文。

Result: 试点评估显示：Precision@5 从 0.56 提升至 0.72，MRR 从 0.43 提升至 0.58，nDCG@5 从 0.51 提升至 0.66。任务中位完成时间从 14.2 分钟降至 8.3 分钟，实用性和置信度均升至 5 分制的 4.0 分。结果基于小样本，作为试点发现报告。

Conclusion: 意图理解和语义丰富能在遗留环境中提供切实的运营价值，同时为未来的分析和自动化工具建立可复用的基础。

Abstract: Energy utilities still run engineering work management, engineering procurement, and inventory processes on long-lived enterprise asset management platforms. Replacing these platforms is often cost prohibitive and operationally disruptive, so practical improvement layers are required. This paper presents a retrieval assistant that improves day-to-day knowledge access across three operational modes: vendor documentation question answering, operational data store (ODS) schema question answering, and user interface usage and how-to question answering. The runtime method combines intent understanding, query rewriting, hybrid semantic and vector retrieval, context engineering under token limits, grounded answer generation, and deterministic hyperlink conversion for panel identifiers and cited documentation. The data preparation pipeline emphasizes semantic enrichment as the primary quality lever by adding table and field descriptions, normalizing acronyms across sources, and indexing representative row-level context when useful. A measured pilot shows consistent gains in retrieval quality and user outcomes. Precision at five improved from 0.56 to 0.72, mean reciprocal rank from 0.43 to 0.58, and normalized discounted cumulative gain (nDCG) at five from 0.51 to 0.66. Median task completion time dropped from 14.2 to 8.3 minutes, while usefulness and confidence both increased to 4.0 on a five-point scale. Results are based on a small sample and are reported as pilot findings, but they indicate that intent understanding and semantic enrichment can deliver meaningful operational value in legacy environments while also establishing reusable foundations for future analytics and automation tools.

</details>


### [227] [Retrieval, not hallucinations, will be the limiting factor for LLM-based clinical AI tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24793)
*Kirk Roberts,Steven Bedrick,Kurt Miller,William R. Hersh,Hongfang Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文为一篇视角文章，呼吁将临床AI中大语言模型错误的讨论重心从精确性错误（如幻觉）转向召回性错误，特别是患者级数据检索中的错误，并系统梳理了错误类型、缓解策略、研究方向及检索评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前临床AI领域对LLM错误的讨论过度聚焦于精确性错误（幻觉），而忽视了召回性错误——即LLM在检索患者级数据时遗漏关键信息的问题，这对许多临床AI工具的可靠性构成更大威胁。

Method: 采用概念分析与文献综述相结合的方式，界定召回性错误的类型（如上下文遗漏、查询理解偏差、索引缺失等），提出缓解策略（改进检索管线、混合检索、人工介入等），并概述了LLM检索领域的研究方向与评估框架。

Result: 文章构建了临床场景下LLM召回性错误的分类体系，给出了针对性的技术缓解路线图，明确了检索增强生成（RAG）系统在临床应用中的关键评估指标与基准需求。

Conclusion: 解决召回性错误是临床AI工具走向可信部署的关键瓶颈，需跨学科协作推进检索系统的鲁棒性评估、标准化基准建设及临床工作流深度融合。

Abstract: Discussions around large language model (LLM) errors in clinical artificial intelligence (AI) generally center around precision errors like hallucinations. This perspective, targeting both clinicians and AI researchers, seeks to shift that discussion to recall errors, particularly in retrieval of patient-level data needed for many clinical AI tools. The perspective outlines types of errors and mitigation strategies, describes research directions in LLMs and retrieval, and provides an overview of retrieval evaluation.

</details>


### [228] [Prediction Is Not Memory: Dual-Timescale Gated Profile Writing for Persistent User Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24798)
*Ziyide Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文针对推荐系统中持久用户画像更新时将交互预测与写入决策混为一谈的问题，提出选择性画像写入控制框架，设计时序远近线下评估协议，并实例化轻量级写入风险门控SPW-Gate，在MicroLens-100K上将远期画像对齐损伤从22.45%降至14.5%，同时保持77%写入覆盖率，证明增益非单纯因写入减少所致，且下一项预测置信度非持久写入有效性的充分代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统常将观测到的交互直接作为持久用户状态更新的证据，忽略交互可能反映瞬时上下文、探索、曝光或短期满足而非稳定偏好形成，导致画像更新引入噪声，损害长期推荐质量。

Method: 1. 形式化定义选择性画像写入控制：交互后决定是否及以何强度写入持久画像。2. 提出时序远近线下协议：近期证据提供弱写入风险监督，远期证据保留用于评估。3. 实例化SPW-Gate轻量级写入风险门控，利用长期、短期及候选画像漂移特征。

Result: 在MicroLens-100K上，全写入更新导致22.45%测试用例远期画像对齐受损；SPW-Gate将远期损伤降至约14.5%，保持约77%写入覆盖率。匹配覆盖率对照及预测置信度基线实验表明，增益非仅因写入减少，且下一项预测置信度不足以作为持久写入有效性的代理指标。

Conclusion: 选择性画像写入控制能有效区分稳定偏好与瞬时交互，SPW-Gate通过多时间尺度特征实现轻量级风险把关，显著降低远期画像对齐损伤且保持高覆盖率，为持久用户画像的鲁棒更新提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Persistent user profiles increasingly serve as reusable memory in recommender systems, but common update pipelines conflate two decisions: predicting an interaction and deciding whether it should persist in the profile. After an interaction is observed, many systems treat it as evidence for updating durable user state. This assumption can be harmful when the event reflects transient context, exploration, exposure, or short-term satisfaction rather than stable preference formation. We formulate this boundary as selective profile-write control: after an observed interaction, a system should decide whether, and how strongly, to write it into the persistent profile. We introduce a chronological near/far offline protocol in which near-future evidence provides weak write-risk supervision and far-future evidence is reserved for evaluation. We instantiate the controller as SPW-Gate, a lightweight write-risk gate using long-term, short-term, and candidate-profile drift features. On MicroLens-100K, write-all updating hurts far-future profile alignment in 22.45% of test cases under the main protocol. SPW-Gate reduces far hurt to about 14.5% while preserving about 77% write coverage. Matched-coverage controls and prediction-confidence baselines show that the gain is not merely a by-product of writing less, and that next-item confidence is not a sufficient proxy for persistent write validity.

</details>


### [229] [Multimodal Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Scientific Document Understanding using Open-Source SLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24799)
*Alexandru-Andrei Saucă,Ana-Luiza Rusnac*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种先进的多模态检索增强生成系统，通过开源视觉语言模型处理表格和图表，结合HNSW语义搜索与GIN词法搜索并经倒数秩融合与交叉编码器重排序，在多轮对话中引入查询冷凝模块，实现了检索质量较朴素RAG基线提升157%、仅增加50ms延迟，且开源小模型性能可媲美云端模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在缺乏微调时回答科学领域问题易产生幻觉；现有检索增强生成方法面临上下文知识有限、稀疏与稠密检索差异及检索噪声等挑战。

Method: 构建多模态摄入管道，利用开源视觉语言模型Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct生成表格和图表的文本摘要；检索阶段融合HNSW稠密语义搜索与GIN稀疏词法搜索，经倒数秩融合后由交叉编码器重排序以降低噪声；引入查询冷凝模块保障多轮对话连贯性。

Result: 在MMLongBench、BeIR格式合成数据集及DeepEval框架上分阶段评估：检索质量较朴素RAG基线提升157%，额外延迟仅50ms；Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct在BERTScore上表现可与云端模型媲美。

Conclusion: 开源优化的小语言模型配合先进检索策略，能在不依赖云端模型的情况下为文档理解提供具有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models tend to hallucinate when answering domain-specific ques tions from scientific documents without prior fine-tuning. Currently, methods such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation partially solve this problem but face different challenges: limited context knowledge, difference between sparse and dense retrieval, and retrieval noise. This paper presents an Advanced Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation system that aims to solve those challenges and im prove the accuracy of information extraction. The proposed architecture introduces a multimodal ingestion pipeline that leverages an open-source Vision-Language Model (Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct) to generate textual summaries of tables and fig ures. The retrieval phase integrates HNSW-based semantic search with GIN-based lexical search, unified through Reciprocal Rank Fusion and refined using Cross Encoder reranking to minimize retrieval noise. To ensure conversational coherence across multi-turn interactions, a Query Condenser module is employed. Evaluation is conducted by independently assessing the ingestion, retrieval and generation stages using the MMLongBench benchmark, a BeIR-format synthetic dataset and the DeepEval framework. Moreover, results demonstrate a 157% improvement in retrieval quality over a Naive-RAG baseline, with only 50 ms additional la tency, while Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct achieved results comparable to cloud-based models in BERTScore. These findings validate that open-source optimized SLMs, paired with advanced retrieval strategies, can provide competitive performance for document understanding without relying on cloud-based models.

</details>


### [230] [When Thinking Before Retrieval Hurts: TraceBound Diagnostics for Adaptive Knowledge-Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24800)
*Partha Sarathi Purkayastha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceBound轻量级诊断协议，分析自适应检索在知识图谱问答中的表现。实验表明，增加条件控制虽提升可检查性，却反而降低检索质量，核心问题在于将“检索前思考”误视为提示格式变更，而非动作选择的控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应检索承诺通过控制器搜索、邻域检查、动作修正和早停机制，增强知识图谱问答的鲁棒性。但该前提缺乏系统性诊断，需揭示自适应检索中常见的失败模式及其根本原因。

Method: 引入TraceBound协议：检索前暴露紧凑查询画像，失败症状出现后发出短轨迹提示，记录轨迹计数器，同时固定图数据、工具、金标签和排序指标。在STaRK验证集和保留子集上对ARK风格检索器进行实证评估。

Result: 条件控制虽改善可检查性，但在开放权重控制器下持续损害检索质量。配对轨迹分析定位性能下降源于重复调用、零结果调用和探索预算错配；收紧交互预算虽缩短轨迹但未修复策略缺陷。

Conclusion: “检索前思考”必须作为动作选择的控制问题来评估，而非单纯的提示格式变更。自适应检索的失败根源在于控制策略的缺陷，需从控制理论角度重新设计检索策略。

Abstract: Adaptive retrieval promises to make knowledge-graph question answering more robust by letting a controller search, inspect neighborhoods, revise actions, and stop when evidence is sufficient. We study this premise by introducing TraceBound, a lightweight profile- and trace-conditioned diagnostic protocol for an ARK-style retriever on text-rich knowledge graphs. TraceBound exposes a compact query profile before retrieval, issues short trace hints after observable failure symptoms, and logs trajectory counters, while keeping graph data, tools, gold labels, and ranking metrics fixed. Across STaRK validation and held-out subsets, the added conditioning improves inspectability but consistently reduces retrieval quality under open-weight controllers. Paired trajectory analysis localizes the degradation to repeated calls, zero-result calls, and misallocated exploration budget, while stricter interaction budgets shorten trajectories without repairing the policy. The result diagnoses the common failure mode in that "thinking before retrieval'' must be evaluated as a control problem over action selection, not as a prompt-format change.

</details>


### [231] [Influence of Prompt Engineering on Small Language Models for Guarded Query Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24801)
*Richard Šléher,William Brach,Kristián Košťál,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究小型语言模型（SLM）在保护查询路由任务中的表现，发现中等规模SLM接近前沿模型性能且延迟更低，提示优化显著提升性能，但极弱模型可能仍需权重级适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究紧凑开放权重的小型语言模型能否在延迟约束下共同处理分布内查询路由和分布外查询拒绝两项任务，即保护查询路由问题。

Method: 在GQR-Bench上评估22个模型，使用分布内准确率和分布外准确率的调和平均（GQR-Score）作为指标；应用提示优化技术（包括少样本提示优化和DSPy签名）无需修改模型权重。

Result: 中等规模SLM接近前沿模型路由质量且延迟更低；许多紧凑模型因输出格式不稳定而失败；提示优化显著提升分数：Mistral 7B从81.79升至90.87，Qwen3.5 9B达95.74（最优优化分数），接近Gemma 3 27B的96.01；Granite 4 Tiny用裸DSPy签名从54.29升至83.05。

Conclusion: 提示优化是保护查询路由的有效首步，但较弱模型可能仍需权重级适应或模式感知训练。

Abstract: We study the problem of guarded query routing, where we assume that a user query first meets a router that either determines the ideal endpoint for in-distribution queries or rejects out-of-distribution queries that are potentially unsafe or out of the system's scope. We investigate whether compact open-weight Small Language Models (SLMs) can jointly handle both tasks under latency constraints. We evaluate 22 models on GQR-Bench and score them with the harmonic mean of in-distribution and out-of-distribution accuracy. We find that mid-scale SLMs come close to frontier model routing quality at much lower latency. Still, many compact models fail because they do not reliably follow the required output format. However, our results show that prompt optimization techniques enable SLMs to handle such cases gracefully, without changing the models' weights. Moreover, few-shot prompt optimization raises Mistral 7B from 81.79 to 90.87 GQR-Score and lifts Qwen3.5 9B to 95.74, the best optimized score in our study and within 0.3 points of the strongest unoptimized larger model: Gemma 3 27B at 96.01. The bare DSPy signature, without in-context exemplars, is the most effective strategy for Granite 4 Tiny, raising its score from 54.29 to 83.05. These results show that prompt optimization is a useful first step for guarded query routing, while weaker models may still need weight-level adaptation or schema-aware training

</details>


### [232] [SourceMinds at CheckThat! 2026: NLI-Grounded Citation Auditing in a Multi-Agent Pipeline for Full Fact-Checking Article Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24802)
*Farhan Sharukh Hasan,Anirban Saha Anik,Eric Liu,Xiaoying Song,Mohotarema Rashid,Lingzi Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体流水线，用于从声明、真实性标签和证据文档生成完整的事实核查文章，结合证据检索、结构化规划、文章生成、门控自我评判和基于NLI的引用审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决事实核查文章生成中证据选择、结构化生成和生成后引用验证的结合问题，提高生成文章的源头依据性和准确性。

Method: 多智能体流水线：1) 密集检索、重排序和源平衡选择进行证据检索；2) 结构化事实规划；3) 基于结构化计划生成带引用的文章；4) 门控自我评判修订弱依据草稿；5) NLI引用审计修复缺失引用并移除不支持或冗余引用。

Result: 系统在CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab Task 3上表现良好，展示了结合证据选择、结构化生成和引用验证的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法强调了证据选择、结构化生成和生成后引用验证相结合对于源头依据的事实核查文章生成的重要性。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for Task 3 of the CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab, which focuses on generating full fact-checking articles from claims, veracity labels, and evidence documents. We propose a multi-agent pipeline that combines evidence retrieval, structured fact planning, article generation, gated self-critique, and NLI-based citation auditing. The system retrieves claim-relevant evidence using dense retrieval, reranking, and source-balanced selection, then generates a citation-supported article from a structured plan. A gated self-critique stage revises weakly grounded drafts, while the NLI citation auditor repairs missing citations and removes unsupported or redundant ones. The approach highlights the importance of combining evidence selection, structured generation, and post-generation citation validation for source-grounded fact-checking article generation.

</details>


### [233] [SciClaimSeekers at CheckThat! 2026: Retrieving Scientific Sources for Social Media Claims with LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24803)
*Mohotarema Rashid,Nansu Baniya,Anirban Saha Anik,Xiaoying Song,Lingzi Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出SciClaimSeekers系统，融合BM25、零样本多语言E5检索与Reciprocal Rank Fusion(k=60)，再经Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct逐点重排序，在CLEF-2026 CheckThat! Task 1英文开发集达到64.36% MRR@5，显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学声明在社交媒体上传播速度远快于验证速度，且帖子极少链接原始学术来源，亟需自动检索相关学术文献以支持声明验证。

Method: 构建SciClaimSeekers流水线：首先用BM25与零样本多语言E5双路检索，再通过Reciprocal Rank Fusion(k=60)融合排序，最后利用Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct进行逐点重排序。

Result: 英文开发集MRR@5达64.36%，较纯BM25提升13.67点，较未重排序的混合检索提升10.17点；官方测试集MRR@5为64.39%。

Conclusion: 精心设计的流水线中组合大型预训练模型，无需任务特定微调即可在科学声明检索任务上达到与微调方法竞争的性能。

Abstract: Scientific claims often spread on social media faster than they can be verified, while posts rarely link to the original scholarly sources. To tackle this problem this paper presents system called SciClaimSeekers, a retrieval and reranking framework by combining BM25 and zero-shot multilingual E5 retrieval with Reciprocal Rank Fusion (k=60), followed by Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct pointwise reranking. The pipeline reaches 64.36% MRR@5 on the English development set a 13.67-point jump over BM25 and 10.17 points over the unranked hybrid and 64.39% on the official test set, in the CLEF-2026 CheckThat! Task 1 evaluation. Our experiment suggests that large pre-trained models, when combined into a careful pipeline, can be competitive with fine-tuned approaches on this task.

</details>


### [234] [Bumblebee: Interleaved Mixed-Layer Building Blocks for Large-Scale Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24804)
*David Bauer,Cancan Zhang,Wenshun Liu,Xiaoyi Zhang,Weijia Liu,Wanli Ma,Yue Weng,Wei Li,Rui Li,Jing Qian,Huayu Li,Xiaoyi Liu,Linhong Zhu,Jerry Fu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Bumblebee是一种推荐架构，通过交错堆叠的模块设计，将序列建模和特征交互两条技术路线深度融合，每个模块包含序列个性化、注意力编码和特征交叉，实现模态早期且反复混合，显著提升工业级推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统发展形成两条分离技术路线：序列建模方法和特征交互方法，缺乏二者深度交互融合，限制了模型表达能力和性能上限。

Method: 提出Bumblebee架构，采用交错可堆叠的模块设计，每个模块实现微流水线：序列个性化层、注意力编码层、特征交叉层，输出联合表示供下一模块使用；模块间残差连接构建跨模态信息路径；支持通过选择性丢弃组件实现质量与吞吐灵活权衡。

Result: 在大规模工业数据上评估，相比可比基线模型在多个分类和回归任务上持续提升；消融实验证实交错组合本身是性能提升主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 交错异构功能单元而非简单加深堆叠，是未来推荐架构演进的有前景范式。

Abstract: Recommendation systems have undergone significant transformations in the past years. The transition from traditional feature interaction modules to generative next-action prediction has pushed the boundaries of personalized content. Developments have largely evolved along two separate tracks. Sequence modeling approaches on the one hand and feature interaction methods on the other. In this paper, we introduce Bumblebee, a recommendation architecture that addresses the lack of interaction between the two directions through an interleaved, stackable block design. Each block implements a micro-pipeline of layers combining sequence personalization, attention-based encoding, and feature crossing into a self-contained unit. Every block produces a joint representation of both feature modalities which is consumed by the next block in the sequence. This mechanism encourages early and repeated mixture of modalities and enriches downstream features with additional contextual information. Residual connections between blocks create cross-modal information pathways and yield additional predictive performance without adding additional parameters. Blocks can be specialized by selectively dropping components, enabling flexible trade-offs between quality and throughput. We evaluate our approach on large-scale industrial data and show consistent improvements over comparable baseline models across several classification and regression tasks. Furthermore, we conduct ablation studies to confirm that the interleaved composition itself is the primary driver of these improvements. Our results suggest that interleaving heterogeneous functional units, rather than composing deep stacks, is a promising paradigm for future-generation recommendation architectures.

</details>


### [235] [Retrieval-Augmented Generation in LLMs for Mental Health: Quantifying the Incremental Contribution of Retrieval Within a Layered Safety Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24817)
*Anand Gupta,Akshat Surolia,Shubham Mishra,Shakil Imtiaz,Chaitali Sinha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文评估了检索增强生成（RAG）在数字心理健康干预（DMHI）Wysa中对大语言模型（LLM）意图检测的影响，发现RAG虽增加误报但显著提升召回率，符合安全优先设计原则，支持RAG作为提升LLM驱动DMHI准确性、一致性和安全性的有效手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DMHI依赖多层安全机制，但单层贡献未量化；纯参数化LLM缺乏安全关键架构，易漏检关键线索或产生幻觉，需验证RAG是否能在波动情境下增强意图检测。

Method: 在Wysa平台对六款LLM进行受控对比实验，分别启用/禁用RAG模式；使用匿名真实与合成用户对话，经临床团队标注多类别意图（自伤、虐待、惊恐等）；计算准确率、召回率、精确率、F1分数并做显著性检验，按风险类别及模型间一致性分析表现。

Result: RAG模式下召回率显著提升，但假阳性增加；误报增多符合安全关键设计中优先敏感性的原则（标记案例转人工复核而非直接干预）；不同风险类别及模型间一致性均有改善。

Conclusion: RAG能有效提升LLM在DMHI中的意图检测准确性、一致性和安全性，尽管会增加误报，但该权衡符合安全优先设计，支持RAG作为改进LLM驱动数字心理健康干预的有前景方案。

Abstract: Digital mental health interventions (DMHIs) offer scalable support, but ensuring they accurately detect users' intent during volatile situations can be challenging. Pure parametric Large Language models (LLMs) do not contain specific safety critical architecture, and can miss critical cues, or hallucinate, undermining reliability. Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG), which supplements an LLM with retrieved context, could enhance intent detection during volatile situations. Commercially available DMHIs typically combine multiple independent safety layers like rule-based filters, symbolic escalation protocols, and neural classification. The incremental contribution of any single layer, however, remains unquantified. This paper evaluates six LLM models within a DMHI called Wysa, via a controlled comparison of RAG-enabled versus RAG-disabled modes. Anonymized real and synthetic user-chatbot exchanges were annotated by a qualified clinical team against multi-class intent categories (e.g. self-harm, abuse, panic). The study computed classification accuracy, recall, precision and F1 scores against ground truth labels and tested differences for statistical significance. Performance was also examined by risk category and inter-model agreement. While RAG caused a rise in false alarms, the trade-off is consistent with safety-critical design principles that prioritize sensitivity, where flagged cases are routed to additional review rather than acted on directly. Overall, these findings support RAG as a promising approach to improve the accuracy, consistency and safety of LLM-driven DMHIs.
  Keywords: Digital Mental Health Intervention, Large Language Model, Retrieval Augmented Generation, Accuracy, Recall, Precision

</details>


### [236] [Dual-Level Atomic and Coordination Geometry Learning for Crystal Property Prediction Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24818)
*Sanjay Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出多尺度图神经网络 CPGN，显式建模配位多面体并耦合原子、键、多面体三层表示，在 Materials Project、JARVIS-DFT、QM9 等基准上超越现有 SOTA GNN，实现高精度、物理可解释的晶体性质预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GNN（如 CGCNN、MEGNet、ALIGNN、SchNet）主要在原子层面表示晶体，通过消息传递隐式学习局部化学环境，但许多材料性质由配位多面体（原子及其邻域形成的基本结构单元）决定，因此需要显式建模配位多面体以提升表示能力和可解释性。

Method: 构建三个耦合图：原子图（编码元素与键合信息）、线图（捕捉角度相互作用）、配位多面体图（基于 Voronoi 划分描述局域环境，包含角共享、棱共享、面共享关系）；为每个多面体引入物理意义的几何描述符；采用交织消息传递机制与双向跨注意力实现跨结构层级的有效信息交换。

Result: 在 Materials Project 上形成能 MAE 0.060 eV/atom、带隙 MAE 0.292 eV；在 JARVIS-DFT 多性质预测具有竞争力；在 QM9 上 HOMO 预测表现优越。整体性能超越现有最先进 GNN 模型。

Conclusion: 显式建模配位多面体能显著改善晶体表示学习，从而实现准确且具有物理可解释性的材料性质预测。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of crystal properties remains a key challenge in computational materials science. While graph neural networks (GNNs) such as CGCNN, MEGNet, ALIGNN, and SchNet have shown strong performance, they primarily represent crystals at the atomic level and implicitly learn local chemical environments through message passing. However, many material properties are governed by coordination polyhedra, the fundamental structural units formed by atoms and their neighboring atoms. To address this limitation, we propose the Coordination Polyhedron Graph Network (CPGN), a multi-scale GNN that jointly learns atomic, bond, and coordination-polyhedron representations. CPGN constructs three coupled graphs: an atom graph encoding elemental and bonding information, a line graph capturing angular interactions, and a coordination polyhedron graph describing Voronoi-derived local environments through corner-, edge-, and face-sharing relationships. Physically meaningful geometric descriptors are incorporated for each polyhedron, while an interleaved message-passing mechanism with bidirectional cross-attention enables effective information exchange across structural levels. Extensive evaluations on the Materials Project, JARVIS-DFT, and QM9 benchmark datasets demonstrate that CPGN outperforms existing state-of-the-art GNN models. It achieves a formation-energy MAE of 0.060 eV/atom and a band-gap MAE of 0.292 eV on the Materials Project, while providing competitive multi-property prediction on JARVIS-DFT and superior HOMO prediction on QM9. The results highlight that explicit modeling of coordination polyhedra improves crystal representation learning and enables accurate, physically interpretable prediction of material properties.

</details>


### [237] [A Position Paper on Recommender Systems in the Era of Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24822)
*Aixin Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该位置论文探讨推荐系统从以人为中心向人机混合环境转型，分析人类、代理和平台三方交互，指出评估、对齐和系统设计面临的新挑战与机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理的快速部署导致推荐系统消费者从单一人类转变为人类与授权代理的混合体，现有以人为中心的RecSys研究亟需适应这一范式转移。

Method: 通过回顾以人为中心的RecSys研究文献，将代理视为独立的、与用户对齐的助手（而非平台内置组件），构建人类-代理-平台三方交互框架，系统梳理三方关系中可能出现的动态机制。

Result: 明确了混合环境下RecSys研究的新机遇（如代理辅助决策、多目标优化等），同时揭示评估指标失效、价值对齐困难、系统架构重构等独特挑战。

Conclusion: 推荐系统必须从单一服务人类转向服务人机混合消费群体，未来研究需重点解决三方交互建模、代理行为评估、跨主体价值对齐等核心问题，推动RecSys向自适应、可信赖的混合智能系统演进。

Abstract: For decades, recommender systems have been optimized to serve human users. However, the rapid deployment of autonomous agents introduces a paradigm shift: recommendation consumers are predicted to be increasingly a mixture of humans and authorized agents acting on their behalf. This position paper reviews insights from prior human-centric RecSys research and outlines the transition to this hybrid environment. In our discussion, we treat agents as independent, user-aligned assistants that act as end-users of recommender systems, rather than platform-built components. We characterize the resulting tripartite interactions among humans, agents, and platforms, highlighting the dynamics that can arise across these relationships. This shift presents new opportunities for RecSys research, while introducing unique challenges for evaluation, alignment, and system design.

</details>


### [238] [Retrieval-based and Fine-tuned LLM Approaches for Industrial Asset Health Monitoring and Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24824)
*Seshu Kumar Damarla,Xiuli Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在FailureSensorIQ基准上评估了检索式方法（TF-IDF、BM25、语义搜索、混合搜索）和开源大语言模型（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的零样本、少样本提示及QLoRA微调）在工业故障传感器诊断推理任务上的表现。结果显示语义/混合搜索优于纯关键词匹配，微调模型效果最佳，但所有方法对选项打乱、标签变更、改述和额外干扰项均敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程师可凭经验诊断机器故障，但将这种推理能力迁移到计算机系统仍具挑战性。本文旨在研究检索式方法和开源大语言模型在FailureSensorIQ多项选择问答基准上的故障-传感器诊断推理性能。

Method: 采用两种路径：1) 检索式方法：将每个答案选项转为选项级查询，利用训练数据中的相似正/负记录打分，对比TF-IDF、BM25、语义搜索、混合搜索四种检索策略。2) LLM方法：使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型，分别进行零样本提示、少样本提示和QLoRA微调实验。

Result: 语义搜索和混合搜索显著优于纯关键词匹配技术，表明基于语义的相似度对工业故障-传感器推理更关键。LLM路径中，微调模型性能最佳，大幅超越零样本和少样本提示。误差分析显示随答案选项数量增加性能下降；鲁棒性分析显示所有方法对选项打乱、标签变更、改述及额外干扰项均敏感。

Conclusion: 基于语义的检索方法和微调后的开源LLM在工业故障-传感器诊断推理任务上展现出潜力，但模型对输入扰动的鲁棒性仍是亟待解决的问题。

Abstract: Industrial plants run many important machines such as pumps, turbines, and compressors. Although engineers can use their experience to identify and diagnose machine problems, transferring this reasoning ability to computer systems remains difficult. This work studies how well a retrieval-only method and an open-source large language model (LLM) perform failure-sensor diagnostic reasoning using the FailureSensorIQ benchmark, a multiple-choice question-answering task introduced by IBM Research. In the retrieval-only approach, each answer option is converted into an option-level query and scored using similar correct and incorrect records from the training data. TF-IDF, BM25, semantic search, and hybrid search are tested and compared. In the LLM-based approach, the Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct model is evaluated using zero-shot prompting, few-shot prompting, and QLoRA fine-tuning. The results show that semantic search and hybrid search perform better than pure keyword-matching techniques, indicating that meaning-based similarity is more important for industrial failure-sensor reasoning. Among the LLM-based methods, the fine-tuned model achieves the best performance and substantially improves over zero-shot and few-shot prompting. Error analysis shows that performance decreases as the number of answer options increases. Robustness analysis also shows that all methods are sensitive to option shuffling, changed labels, paraphrasing, and additional distractors.

</details>


### [239] [Aethel: A Reproducible Graph-Retrieval Framework for Multi-Hop Financial Diligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24826)
*Krish Sapru*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Aethel框架，结合二分图Personalized PageRank检索与共指感知的二分图共指传送层及专家代理架构，用于二级私募股权交易中的多跳财务尽职调查。实验表明，该方法在2WikiMultiHopQA上HR@5达100%，在MuSiQue上达88.5%，优于普通图检索；在金融披露语料上，图检索在多跳召回上优于密集检索，但未超越BM25基线。结果显示图检索提供可解释的多跳证据路径，随语料规模增长性能下降更平缓，但优势强依赖语料规模和实体索引质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二级私募股权交易需快速综合分散、非结构化的财务披露，关键指标及其实体锚点分布在互不相连的文档中，词汇重叠有限，现有检索方法难以有效支持多跳财务尽职调查。

Method: 提出Aethel框架：将语料建模为实体-段落二分图，采用二分图Personalized PageRank进行图检索，引入共指感知的二分图共指传送层处理共指消解，并设计编排专家代理架构支持多跳推理。对比稀疏词法、密集双编码器、普通图检索及共指感知图检索。

Result: 在2WikiMultiHopQA验证集上HR@5达到100%，在MuSiQue上达到88.5%，显著提升覆盖率但牺牲顶部精度。在4,123块金融披露语料上，图检索在多跳召回上优于密集检索，但在开放语料规模下未超越强BM25基线。图检索提供可解释的多跳证据路径，随语料增大性能下降更平缓，优势强依赖语料规模和实体索引质量。

Conclusion: 图基检索为多跳财务尽职调查提供可解释的证据路径，且随语料规模增长鲁棒性优于密集检索，但其优势受限于语料规模和实体索引质量，未来工作需进一步优化大规模开放语料下的检索效果。

Abstract: Secondary private equity transactions require rapid synthesis of fragmented, unstructured financial disclosures, where critical metrics and their entity anchors are distributed across disjoint documents with limited lexical overlap. We present Aethel, a reproducible framework that combines bipartite Personalized PageRank graph retrieval with a coreference-aware Bipartite Coreference Teleportation layer and an orchestrated specialist-agent architecture. Aethel models corpora as entity-passage graphs and propagates relevance through explicit relational paths to support multi-hop financial diligence. We evaluate the retrieval layer on 200-question samples from the MuSiQue and 2WikiMultiHopQA validation sets, comparing sparse lexical, dense bi-encoder, vanilla graph, and coreference-aware graph retrieval. Aethel achieves HR@5 of 100.0% on 2WikiMultiHopQA and 88.5% on MuSiQue, improving coverage over vanilla graph retrieval while trading off top-rank precision. We also evaluate retrieval over a 4,123-chunk corpus of financial disclosures and find that graph retrieval outperforms dense retrieval on multi-hop recall but does not surpass a strong BM25 baseline at open-corpus scale. The results show that graph-based retrieval offers interpretable multi-hop evidence paths and degrades more gracefully than dense retrieval as corpus size grows, while also demonstrating that its advantage depends strongly on corpus scale and entity-index quality. Code and evaluation artifacts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [240] [Improving Rare Medication Recommendation with Counterfactual Data Augmentation and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24829)
*Shinhwan Kang,Soo Yong Lee,Jaewon Kim,Kijung Shin,Buru Chang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GenRxR框架，利用大语言模型生成反事实数据缓解罕用药数据稀缺，并通过指令微调建模共用药关系，显著提升罕用药推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有药物推荐方法在罕用药预测性能较差，主要由于罕用药数据稀缺和缺乏对共用药关系的建模。

Method: 基于大语言模型的GenRxR框架，利用LLM生成反事实医疗数据缓解数据稀缺，整合LLM建模共用药关系，并引入指令微调对齐推荐任务。

Result: 在实验中超越14个基线（含5个基于LLM的），罕用药预测性能最高提升30.9%。

Conclusion: GenRxR有效解决了罕用药推荐中的数据稀缺和共用药建模问题，显著提升了罕用药推荐准确性。

Abstract: AI-based medication recommendation systems have attracted substantial attention due to their potential to enhance patient safety and therapeutic outcomes. Despite the clinical importance of accurately recommending rarely prescribed medications (rare-meds), we observe that most existing methods show significantly lower predictive performance for rare-meds. We attribute this issue to two intrinsic limitations: (a) the inherent scarcity of data for rare-meds and (b) limited consideration of co-recommended medications. To address these limitations, we propose GenRxR, a novel framework based on large language models (LLMs). GenRxR leverages the medical knowledge and clinical reasoning capability of LLMs to generate counterfactual medical data, mitigating the data scarcity issue for rare-meds. It also integrates an LLM into the medication recommendation process to model relationships among co-recommended medications. To further enhance the clinical reasoning, we introduce an instruction tuning step that aligns the LLM's capability with the recommendation task, enabling better handling of clinical context, including rare-meds cases. In our experiments, we show that GenRxR outperforms 14 (including 5 LLM-based) baselines in most cases. Specifically, it achieves up to 30.9% higher predictive performance for rare-meds than the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [241] [MedJudgeRAG: Option-Wise Evidence Judgment with Dynamic Knowledge Graphs for Medical MCQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24838)
*Seongwon Seo,Seung Hwan Cho,Young-Min Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 MedJudgeRAG，一种用于医学多项选择题问答的检索增强生成框架，通过动态知识图谱和证据裁决机制改进知识利用策略，在两个医学基准上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RAG 方法无差别地利用检索文档，可能降低语言模型性能，需要更精细的证据判断和知识利用策略。

Method: 将检索文档表示为实体和关系组成的动态知识图谱；为每个选项从文档和图谱中判断证据裁决；基于裁决组合确定知识利用策略推理最终答案；通过教师模型生成的结构化推理轨迹进行监督微调，采用加权交叉熵损失差异化加权图谱和推理片段。

Result: 在两个医学多项选择题基准上，MedJudgeRAG 持续优于 vanilla RAG 和参数化基线；消融实验表明动态知识图谱作为训练时的图条件监督比作为推理时的显式输出更有效。

Conclusion: MedJudgeRAG 通过动态知识图谱和证据裁决机制，有效提升了医学多项选择题问答的准确性，且图谱在训练阶段作为监督信号最为关键。

Abstract: In medical multiple-choice question answering (MCQA), Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) can supplement the domain knowledge of language models (LMs). However, since vanilla RAG indiscriminately utilizes retrieved documents, it can degrade LM performance. To address this, we propose MedJudgeRAG. Our framework represents retrieved documents as a dynamic knowledge graph (KG) composed of entities and relations. For each option, the model judges an evidence verdict from the retrieved documents and the KG. Based on the verdict combination, the model determines a knowledge utilization strategy to reason toward the final answer. These capabilities are trained via supervised fine-tuning using structured reasoning traces generated by a teacher LM. The training employs a weighted cross-entropy loss that differentially weights the KG and reasoning segments. Experiments on two medical MCQA benchmarks demonstrate that MedJudgeRAG consistently outperforms both vanilla RAG and parametric baselines. Furthermore, ablation analysis reveals that the dynamic KG is more effective as graph-conditioned supervision at training time than as an explicit output at inference time. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyu-amllab/medjudgerag, and the generated reasoning traces are released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/youarethewon/medjudgerag.

</details>


### [242] [REPREC: Representation Driven Parameter-Efficient Recommendation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24845)
*Harshini Kavuru,Dwipam Katariya,Giri Iyengar,Pranab Mohanty,Kalanand Mishra,Kalanand Mishra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出REPREC，一种轻量级框架，通过冻结的序列编码器获取用户嵌入，经轻量MLP映射为软令牌注入冻结LLM，仅训练注入器，实现模块化、生产友好的序列推荐，性能超越LoRA且训练效率提升1.51倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的序列推荐方法常依赖微调、额外架构模块、表征蒸馏或长历史条件化，增加训练复杂度和部署成本，需一种轻量、模块化且不修改预训练组件的解决方案。

Method: REPREC从冻结的序列编码器提取定长用户嵌入，通过轻量MLP注入器映射为少量可学习软令牌，条件化冻结的LLM生成推荐；仅训练注入器，保持两个预训练主干不变，兼容不同编码器和LLM主干。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，REPREC持续超越LoRA，对休闲和核心用户增益显著；在短历史训练、长上下文评估设定下保持LoRA 85-100%性能，人均每轮训练时间降低1.51倍，展示推荐质量与计算效率的有效平衡。

Conclusion: REPREC通过轻量用户表征对齐实现了免微调、模块化、生产友好的LLM序列推荐，尤其在低数据 regimes 表现优异，为工业部署提供了高效可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been applied to sequential recommendation by formulating it as a natural language task. Previous work has improved personalization by incorporating collaborative and sequential signals through input conditioning or LLM fine-tuning. However, existing approaches often rely on one or more of the following: LLM fine-tuning, additional architectural modules, representation distillation, or item-level conditioning over long interaction histories, increasing training complexity and deployment cost. We propose REPREC, a lightweight framework that reformulates LLM-based sequential recommendation through lightweight user representation alignment. REPREC maps a fixed-size user embedding from a frozen sequential encoder into a small set of learned soft tokens through a lightweight MLP injector that conditions a frozen LLM, leaving both pretrained backbones unchanged while training only the injector. We conducted exhaustive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets and demonstrate that REPREC consistently outperforms LoRA while remaining compatible with different pretrained sequential encoders and LLM backbones, enabling a modular and production-friendly recommendation pipeline without modifying either pretrained component. The gains are particularly pronounced for casual and core users across all datasets, highlighting REPREC's effectiveness in low-data regimes. Finally, when trained on short prompt histories and evaluated with longer contexts, REPREC maintains 85-100% of LoRA's performance while reducing per-epoch training time by an average of 1.51X, demonstrating an effective balance between recommendation quality and computational efficiency for production deployment. The code is available at https://github.com/phdbotcode/REPREC

</details>


### [243] [Two Views, One Voice: Evidence-Grounded Conversational Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24846)
*Sungwook Yoo,Sewook Yoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种解耦检索与响应生成的对话推荐系统，在ACM RecSys Challenge 2026 Blind-B赛道获得第三名，并在解释质量榜单上排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对话推荐系统将检索和响应生成耦合在单一文本接口中，导致实体线索随对话意图演变而淡化，损害解释可信度。比赛要求同时进行Top-20排序和基于证据的响应生成。

Method: 将检索和响应解耦为独立流水线，通过排序轨迹和元数据连接。检索结合混合词法-稠密池（精确匹配）和任务适配池（微调Qwen 8B适配器）。候选项经LightGBM校准，再路由到基于证据的Propose-Assign-Select (PAS) 框架构建响应。

Result: 该系统在Blind-B行业赛道获得第三名，并在最终盲测评估的解释质量排行榜上排名第二。

Conclusion: 隔离检索与响应生成能同时保留目录线索和流动意图；通过显式证据分配结构化生成是实现近最优解释可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Traditional conversational recommenders entangle retrieval and response generation within a single text interface, so exact entity cues fade as the dialogue's intent evolves, which compromises explanation credibility. We address this within the ACM RecSys Challenge 2026, which mandates both top-20 ranking and evidence-grounded response generation. This paper presents the third-place solution by team "swyoo" for the Blind-B industry track. We decouple retrieval and response into separate pipelines connected strictly via ranked tracks and metadata. Retrieval combines a hybrid lexical-dense pool for exact matching with a task-adapted pool driven by fine-tuned Qwen 8B adapters. Candidates are calibrated via LightGBM, then routed to an evidence-grounded propose-assign-select (PAS) framework to structure responses. This system also ranked second on the explanation-quality leaderboard in the final blind evaluation. Our findings demonstrate that: (i) isolating retrieval and response preserves both catalog cues and fluid intent; (ii) structuring generation via explicit evidence assignment is key to this near-best-in-class explanation reliability.

</details>


### [244] [SearchArt: Training Long-Horizon Search Agent with Scalable Synthetic and Verified Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24850)
*Lang Mei,Xiaohan Yu,Chong Chen,Liyan Liu,Xiangnan Chen,Jinchao Ma,Chao Feng,Li Huang,Siyu Mo,Sichen Kang,Yunkun Xu,Zhihan Yang,Zhujun Xue,Jingren Zhang,Qing He,Yingdi Huang,Hao Jiang,Ziao Ma,Zewei Pan,Minhao Sun,Zhuo Tao,Jinzhao Xiao,Gangtao Xin,Huanyao Zhang,Wenjian Zhang,Jiangshan Zhang,Guojie Zhu,Jiaxin Mao,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SearchArt是一个可扩展框架，用于训练长视野搜索智能体。通过验证驱动的任务合成和多阶段后训练流程，构建大规模复杂搜索任务数据集，结合监督微调和强化学习策略优化，仅用27B参数模型即在多个基准上达到或超越闭源智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型驱动的搜索智能体在长视野复杂任务上表现不足，主要受限于缺乏可扩展的长视野任务数据，以及难以评估和纠正中间推理与工具使用行为。需要一种能自动生成高质量训练数据并支持长程交互训练的框架。

Method: 提出SearchArt框架：1) 从网页文档和自动生成的证据图中合成多样化的信息搜索问答对及对应搜索轨迹；2) 设计验证管道，联合评估问答一致性、轨迹质量和检索证据相关性；3) 利用验证后的轨迹进行多阶段训练，包括监督微调和基于强化学习的策略优化。

Result: 在仅使用（Qwen3.5-）27B参数模型的情况下，SearchArt在BrowseComp-ZH上得分74.39，BrowseComp上70.06，Deepresearch-bench上52.55，在深度搜索和深度研究基准上均达到或超越前沿闭源智能体。

Conclusion: SearchArt通过可扩展的任务合成和验证驱动的数据构建，结合多阶段后训练，成功训练出具备自适应搜索规划、迭代证据聚合和长程复杂推理能力的搜索智能体，证明了小参数模型也能在长视野搜索任务上达到顶尖水平。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled search agents to autonomously tackle complex tasks across extended search and reasoning horizons. However, training effective search agents remains challenging due to the lack of scalable and long-horizon tasks, and the difficulty of evaluating and correcting intermediate reasoning and tool-use behaviors. We introduce SearchArt, a scalable framework for training long-horizon search agents through verification-driven task synthesis and a multi-stage post-training pipeline. SearchArt constructs large-scale datasets for complex search-, research- and user-oriented tasks by synthesizing diverse information-seeking QA pairs and corresponding search trajectories from web documents and automatically generated evidence graphs. To ensure the reliability of the synthesized data, we design a verification pipeline that jointly evaluates QA consistency, trajectory quality, and the relevance of retrieved evidence. The verified trajectories are subsequently used in a multi-stage training process comprising supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning-based policy optimization. Search agents trained with SearchArt exhibit adaptive search planning, iterative evidence aggregation, and complex reasoning over extended interaction horizons. Experimental results demonstrate that, with only (Qwen3.5-) 27B parameters, SearchArt scores 74.39 on BrowseComp-ZH, 70.06 on BrowseComp, and 52.55 on Deepresearch-bench, matching or surpassing frontier closed-source agents on both deepsearch and deepresearch benchmarks.

</details>


### [245] [HVM-GraphRAG: Holistic-View Multimodal Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Complex Document](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24861)
*Xin He,Yili Wang,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li,Qinggang Zhang,Yi Chang,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HVM-GraphRAG框架，通过整体视角指导图构建，减少噪声和冲突，建立可靠的跨模态索引，在概念级图上检索并直接访问证据，避免昂贵的实体级图遍历，最后将检索到的块按模态分组以便更好地整合异构证据，在三个数据集上实现最佳回答性能并显著提升检索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态GraphRAG方法在复杂文档问答中存在跨模态证据索引不可靠和图遍历开销大的问题，需要一种能够可靠索引跨模态证据并高效检索的框架。

Method: HVM-GraphRAG采用整体视角指导图构建，减少噪声和冲突的图更新，在概念级图节点与支持的多模态块之间建立可靠索引；检索时在紧凑的概念级图上搜索并通过索引直接访问证据，避免在稠密实体级图上昂贵的遍历；检索后将块重组为模态特定组，便于回答模型整合异构证据。

Result: 在三个数据集上实验，HVM-GraphRAG在大多数评估设置中达到最佳回答性能，同时相比代表性的基于图的基线显著提升在线检索效率。

Conclusion: HVM-GraphRAG通过整体视角指导的图构建和高效的概念级检索，解决了跨模态索引不可靠和图遍历昂贵的问题，在复杂文档问答中实现了最优性能和高效率。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over complex documents requires models to retrieve and integrate evidence distributed across distant document regions and modalities. Multimodal GraphRAG provides a promising direction by organizing document evidence with graph structures. However, existing methods often suffer from unreliable cross-modal evidence indexing and expensive graph traversal. To address these issues, we propose HVM-GraphRAG, a holistic-view multimodal GraphRAG framework on complex document. HVM-GraphRAG uses a holistic view to guide graph construction, thereby reducing noisy and conflicting graph updates and building reliable indices between concept-level graph nodes and supporting multimodal chunks. During retrieval, HVM-GraphRAG searches over a compact concept-level graph and directly accesses supporting evidence through the constructed index, avoiding costly traversal over dense entity-level graphs. After obtaining the retrieved evidence, HVM-GraphRAG further reorganizes chunks into modality-specific groups, enabling the answering model to better integrate heterogeneous evidence. Experiments on three datasets show that HVM-GraphRAG achieves the best answer performance in most evaluated settings while substantially improving online retrieval efficiency over representative graph-based baselines.

</details>


### [246] [KuaiLive-M3: A Multi-Modal, Multi-Domain, and Multi-Feedback Dataset for Live Streaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24862)
*Ke Guo,Changle Qu,Jiayaqi Cheng,Xiao Zhang,Shijun Wang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Xueliang Wang,Le Zhang,Lantao Hu,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出KuaiLive-M3数据集，解决现有直播推荐数据集的三大局限：缺乏时序演变多模态内容、忽视短视频与直播的跨域交互、仅含隐式行为无显式反馈。数据集包含21,938用户、35M直播交互、111M短视频交互、88M时间戳段级多模态嵌入及25,403份问卷反馈，建立跨域推荐、直播高光预测、问卷增强推荐三大基准，实验证明其为极具挑战性和真实性的基准，强调建模时序内容、跨域偏好迁移、隐显式反馈融合的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开直播数据集存在三大缺陷：1) 有限访问时序演变的多模态直播内容；2) 忽略用户在短视频与直播间的跨域交互；3) 仅含隐式行为信号，缺乏捕捉用户感知内容质量和满意度的显式反馈。这些局限阻碍了对真实直播场景的忠实反映和全面研究。

Method: 从快手平台收集KuaiLive-M3数据集，包含：21,938用户，35M直播交互，111M短视频交互，细粒度时间戳和多样行为；约88M带时间戳的段级多模态嵌入，捕捉直播内容时序演变；25,403份问卷反馈记录，连接隐式行为与显式偏好。基于这些信号建立三大基准任务：跨域推荐、直播高光预测、问卷增强推荐，并用代表性基线进行大量实验。

Result: 大量实验表明KuaiLive-M3为未来直播推荐研究提供了极具挑战性和真实性的基准。结果突显了建模时序演变内容、跨域迁移用户偏好、弥合隐式行为与显式用户反馈鸿沟的重要性。数据集和基准代码已公开。

Conclusion: KuaiLive-M3填补了现有直播推荐数据集的关键空白，通过引入多模态时序内容、跨域交互数据和显式问卷反馈，构建了更贴近真实场景的综合基准，为直播推荐研究提供了宝贵资源，推动了时序建模、跨域迁移和隐显式反馈融合等方向的发展。

Abstract: Existing public live streaming datasets suffer from three major limitations: they provide limited access to temporally evolving multimodal live content, overlook users' cross-domain interactions between short videos and live streams, and contain only implicit behavioral signals without explicit feedback that captures users' perceived content quality and satisfaction. These limitations prevent existing benchmarks from faithfully reflecting real-world live streaming scenarios and hinder comprehensive research on live streaming recommendation. To address these limitations, we introduce KuaiLive-M3, a multi-modal, multi-domain, and multi-feedback dataset for live streaming recommendation, collected from Kuaishou, a leading live streaming and short video platform in China. KuaiLive-M3 covers 21,938 users and contains 35 million live streaming interactions and 111 million short video interactions, with fine-grained timestamps and diverse user behaviors. It further provides approximately 88 million timestamped segment-level multi-modal embeddings that capture the temporal evolution of live streaming content, as well as 25,403 questionnaire-based feedback records that bridge implicit user behaviors and explicit user preferences. Based on these unique signals, we establish benchmarks for cross-domain recommendation, live stream highlight prediction, and questionnaire-enhanced recommendation. Extensive experiments with representative baselines demonstrate that KuaiLive-M3 provides a challenging and realistic benchmark for future live streaming recommendation research. The results further highlight the importance of modeling temporally evolving content, transferring user preferences across domains, and bridging the gap between implicit behaviors and explicit user feedback. The dataset and benchmark code are publicly available at https://imgkkk574.github.io/KuaiLive-M3/.

</details>


### [247] [Tokens are All You Need: Dual-purpose Semantic IDs for Achieving LLM-Level I/O Efficiency in recommendation systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24865)
*Baolei Li,Yiping Yuan,Yilin Zheng,Likang Yin,Ling Liu,Fabio Soldo,Romer Rosales,Xinyang Yi,Lichan Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出双重用途语义ID，通过层次化量化将连续嵌入压缩为离散标记，同时作为协同身份和内容重建，大幅降低推荐系统存储和I/O开销，在大规模视频平台验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推荐系统面临“内存墙”瓶颈，海量稠密嵌入表导致存储和I/O效率低下；生成式检索虽用离散token表示ID，但高维上下文仍依赖低效稠密格式，亟需兼顾效率与表达能力的离散化方案。

Method: 提出双重用途语义ID，采用层次化量化将连续嵌入压缩为离散语义标记，同时承担两大功能：(1) 协同身份：通过可学习嵌入表建模用户-物品交互；(2) 内容重建：利用轻量级语义解码器实时近似嵌入，实现按需重建替代海量向量存储。

Result: 离线评估和在大型视频分享平台的生产级排序与检索系统在线部署均验证了框架有效性，证明离散标记足以支撑高效、内容丰富的推荐。

Conclusion: 双重用途语义ID通过离散化实现LLM级I/O效率，解决了推荐系统内存墙问题，为大规模推荐提供了存储-计算权衡的新范式。

Abstract: Large-scale recommendation systems face "Memory Wall" bottlenecks due to massive, dense embedding tables. While generative retrieval uses discrete tokens for IDs, high-dimensional context still relies on inefficient dense formats. Inspired by computer vision data compression, we propose Dual-purpose Semantic IDs to achieve LLM-level I/O efficiency. Our methodology uses hierarchical quantization to condense continuous embeddings into discrete Semantic IDs performing two concurrent roles: (1) Collaborative Identity: modeling user-item interactions via learnable embedding table; and (2) Content Reconstruction: using a lightweight Semantic Decoder for on-the-fly embedding approximation. This approach replaces massive vector storage with on-demand reconstruction, reducing system overhead and data footprints. We demonstrate the efficacy of our framework through offline evaluations and successful online deployment in production-scale ranking and retrieval systems at a major video sharing platform, showing that discrete tokens are indeed all you need for highly efficient, content-rich recommendation.

</details>


### [248] [Ranked by Position: Order Sensitivity as an Exploitable Attack Surface in LLM Listwise Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24869)
*Ge Zhang,Jingru Cheng,Huiyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大语言模型（LLM）作为列表式重排序器在推荐系统中的位置偏差问题，发现攻击者可通过重排候选项顺序将标签为0的目标提升至top-k，提出promo@k指标量化漏洞，实验显示promo@5高达0.57，并提出双向T5编码器、排列一致性正则化和架构不变性等缓解方案，指出输入候选顺序是安全相关的攻击向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推荐系统中作为列表式重排序器时，将候选集序列化为提示词会引入位置偏差，这种顺序敏感性可能被攻击者利用，在不改变项目内容、标签或模型参数的情况下操纵排名，因此需要量化并缓解这一安全风险。

Method: 提出promo@k指标衡量漏洞，即通过排列使标签为0的目标进入top-k的比例；在MovieLens、Amazon Books、Amazon Fashion三个领域评估，攻击预算R=50；分析普通排列稳定性预测漏洞；对比双向T5编码器评分器、排列一致性正则化、架构不变性及逐点评分等缓解方法。

Result: promo@5最高达0.57（R=50），表明漏洞严重；排列稳定性可预测漏洞无需运行攻击；双向T5编码器降低暴露，排列一致性正则化和架构不变性有效缓解；逐点评分虽避免偏差但降低排序质量。

Conclusion: 列表式LLM重排序中的输入候选顺序是一个与安全相关的攻击向量，需通过架构设计和正则化来增强鲁棒性，平衡排序质量和安全性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) used as listwise rerankers in recommendation systems suffer from position bias when serializing candidate sets into prompts. We show this order sensitivity creates an exploitable attack surface: an attacker can promote a label-0 target into the top-$k$ solely by reordering candidates, without changing item content, labels, or model parameters. We introduce $\mathrm{promo}@k$ to quantify this vulnerability, measuring the fraction of label-0 targets that can be elevated into top-$k$ rankings via permutation. Evaluating across three domains (MovieLens, Amazon Books, and Amazon Fashion), $\mathrm{promo}@5$ reaches up to 0.57 at an attack budget of $R$ = 50 orderings. Furthermore, ordinary permutation stability predicts vulnerability without running the attack. While a bidirectional T5 encoder scorer reduces exposure, permutation-consistency regularization and architectural invariance effectively mitigate it. Pointwise scoring avoids the bias issue but degrades ranking quality. These results demonstrate that input candidate order in listwise LLM reranking is a security-relevant attack vector. Code and data are available at https://github.com/geoz-lab/position_bias_attack.

</details>


### [249] [Agent Retrieval Bench: Evaluating Repository Context Retrieval for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24882)
*Bowen Qin,Yi Xie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 Agent Retrieval Bench，一个文件级检索基准，用于评估编码代理上下文获取阶段的检索能力，包含四个正向检索任务和一个选择性检索子集，评估多种检索方法，发现没有单一方法全面优势，且存在校准差距和代理轨迹遗漏金标准文件的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理评估主要关注最终补丁生成，但补丁生成依赖于上游的上下文获取阶段（即找到任务所需的仓库文件），缺乏针对这一检索问题的专门基准。

Method: 基于真实编码工作流信号构建样本，针对冻结的基础提交仓库评估，相关性定义为代理接下来需要的内容而非直接查询-文件语义相似性。基准包含四个正向检索任务（code2test、comment2context、trace2code、edit2ripple）和一个选择性检索子集，共 427 个样本，跨 25 个仓库，语料库含 308 个基础提交快照、39.2 万文件、790 万代码块。评估词法检索、RepoMap、开源嵌入模型、选择性弃权和记录的代理上下文选择。

Result: 没有单一检索系列全面占优：Qwen3-Embedding-4B 在正样本上样本加权 MRR 最佳，Qwen3-Embedding-8B Recall@20 最佳，RepoMap 在 8K token 预算下上下文产出最佳，任务级胜者差异大。用反事实控制校准的选择性阈值未提升自然无金标准情况下的选择性成功，揭示校准差距。记录的代理轨迹在 27-35% 样本上完全遗漏所有金标准文件。受控种子干预实验显示，检索派生的初始上下文比随机非金上下文产生更高的文件 F1 且需更少后续探索，而 oracle 金标准上下文仍有巨大提升空间。
